\displaystyle=\min\bigg\{k:\beta_{k}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{\min}(Q_{22})}}{2(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}})\sqrt{\gamma_{\max}(Q_{22})}},

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\leq\frac{a_{22}/2}{(\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}+1)(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}})}\penalty\ \forall k\geq k_{C}\bigg\};

\displaystyle k_{1}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{k:\frac{a_{22}}{2}\geq\frac{1-\xi}{\alpha(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}}\penalty\ \forall k\geq k_{L}\right\};

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle=\max\{d_{x},d_{y}\};

\displaystyle b_{max}

\displaystyle=\max_{j\in\{1,2\}}\max_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|b_{j}^{(i)}(o^{\prime})\right|,\text{where }b_{j}^{(i)}(o^{\prime})\text{is the }i^{\prime}th\text{element of the vector }b_{j}(o^{\prime});

\displaystyle\kappa_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}};\penalty\ \penalty\ \kappa_{Q_{\Delta}}=\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta})}};\penalty\ \penalty\ \kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}=\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta,\beta})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta,\beta})}};

\displaystyle A_{max}

\displaystyle=\max_{o\in\mathcal{S}}\left\{\max_{i,j\in\{1,2\}}\{\|A_{ij}(o)\|\}\right\};\penalty\ \penalty\ \varrho_{x}=\kappa_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|;\penalty\ \penalty\ \varrho_{y}=\|\Delta\|+\|A_{12}\|\kappa_{Q_{22}}.

In this paper, our aim is to establish the dependency of the second order term in terms of the dimension of the variables
x_{k}
and
y_{k}
. For doing so, we will keep track of all the constants in the paper which we assume to be independent of the dimension. Specifically, we will assume that matrix operator norms and eigenvalues of various matrices do not scale with the dimension. For example, the following constants are assumed to be dimension independent:
a_{22},\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}},\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}},\sqrt{\gamma_{\min}(Q_{22})},\sqrt{\gamma_{\max}(Q_{22})}
, etc. Also, note that the mixing constant
\rho
may also contribute to the dimensional dependence, but we do not study that here.

Before starting the proof of the main results, we will state some properties of the matrix
\Sigma^{x},\Sigma^{xy}
and
\Sigma^{y}
given in Eqs. (
4.4a
)-(
4.4c
) which will be used extensively. Firstly, observe that
\|b_{i}(o)\|\leq b_{max}\sqrt{d},\penalty\ i\in\{1,2\}
. Let
U_{1}J_{1}U_{1}^{-1}=A_{22}
be the Jordan canonical decomposition of
A_{22}
. The Lyapunov equation is given by:

\displaystyle A_{22}\Sigma^{x}+\Sigma^{x}A_{22}^{\top}=\Gamma^{x}

where
\Gamma^{x}=\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\|\Gamma^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}\|+\|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]\|}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}\|+2\|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\|}

\displaystyle\leq b_{max}^{2}d+\frac{8\tau_{mix}}{3}b_{max}^{2}d

(Lemma
D.15
)

\displaystyle\leq 4b_{max}^{2}d\tau_{mix}

(
\tau_{mix}\geq 1
)

Define the following:

\displaystyle\sigma^{x}=4b^{2}_{max}\|U_{1}\|\|U_{1}^{-1}\|\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m_{A_{22}}}{n+n^{\prime}\choose k}\frac{1}{(-2r_{A_{22}})^{n+n^{\prime}+1}},

where
m_{A_{22}}
is the largest algebraic multiplicity of the matrix
A_{22}
and
r_{A_{22}}=\max_{i}\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]
, where
\lambda_{i}
is the
i
-th eigen value. Then, using Lemma
D.16
, we have
\|\Sigma^{x}\|\leq\sigma^{x}d\tau_{mix}
.

\displaystyle A_{12}\Sigma^{x}+\Sigma^{xy}A_{22}^{\top}=\Gamma^{xy}

where
\Gamma^{xy}=\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}
. Similar to bounding to
\Gamma^{x}
, we get
\|\Gamma^{xy}\|\leq 4b_{max}^{2}d\tau_{mix}
. Define
\sigma^{xy}=\|A_{22}^{-1}\|\left(4b_{max}^{2}+\|A_{12}\|\sigma^{x}\right)
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\Sigma^{xy}\|\leq\sigma^{xy}d\tau_{mix}.

Finally, note that
\Sigma^{y}
is the solution to the following Lyapunov equation:

\displaystyle\left(\Delta-\frac{\beta^{-1}I}{2}\right)\Sigma^{y}+\Sigma^{y}\left(\Delta^{\top}-\frac{\beta^{-1}I}{2}\right)=\Gamma^{y}-A_{12}\Sigma^{yx}-\Sigma^{xy}A_{12}^{\top}.

Similar to bounding to
\Gamma^{x}
, we get
\|\Gamma^{y}\|\leq 4b_{max}^{2}d\tau_{mix}
. From the previous bounds we can bound the norm of the r.h.s as follows:

\displaystyle\|\Gamma^{y}-A_{12}\Sigma^{yx}-\Sigma^{xy}A_{12}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\Gamma^{y}\|+2\|A_{12}\|\|\Sigma^{yx}\|

\displaystyle\leq b_{max}^{2}d+\frac{8\tau_{mix}}{3}b_{max}^{2}d+2\|A_{12}\|\|A_{22}^{-1}\|\left(4b_{max}^{2}+\sigma^{x}\right)d\tau_{mix}

\displaystyle\leq\left(4b_{max}^{2}+2\|A_{12}\|\sigma^{xy}\right)d\tau_{mix}

Assume
U_{2}J_{2}U_{2}^{-1}=\Delta-\frac{\beta^{-1}I}{2}
to be Jordan canonical decomposition of
\Delta-\frac{\beta^{-1}I}{2}
.

\displaystyle\sigma^{y}=\left(4b_{max}^{2}+2\|A_{12}\|\sigma^{xy}\right)\|U_{2}\|\|U_{2}^{-1}\|\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m_{\Delta,\beta}}{n+n^{\prime}\choose k}\frac{1}{(-2r_{\Delta,\beta})^{n+n^{\prime}+1}},

where
m_{\Delta,\beta}
is the largest algebraic multiplicity of the matrix
A
and
r_{\Delta,\beta}=\max_{i}\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]
, where
\lambda_{i}
is the
i
-th eigen value. Then, using Lemma
D.16
, we have
\|\Sigma^{y}\|\leq\sigma^{y}d\tau_{mix}
.

Before we start the proof, we give a schematic road map of the proof of Theorem
4.1
in Figure
4
. Recall that the proof of our main lemma
D.2
that pillars our theorem is based upon induction argument. Thus, we have divided the auxiliary lemmas into two groups: Induction dependent lemmas that are proved using the induction hypothesis and Induction independent lemmas that proved using only the problem structure and assumptions.

Theorem
4.1
: Use the linear transformation to show that the coupled iterates satisfy the following

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+C^{x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+C^{xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}+C^{y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y}

where
\max\{\|C^{x}_{k}\|,\|C^{z}_{k}\|,\|C^{y}_{k}\|\}\leq c_{0}(\varrho,d)
for all
k\geq 0
.

Lemma
D.1
: Use Lemma
D.2
to show that the decoupled iterates satisfy the following

\displaystyle\left[\tilde{X}_{k},\tilde{Z}_{k},\tilde{Y}_{k}\right]=\left[\alpha_{k}(\Sigma^{x}+o(1)),\beta_{k}(\Sigma^{xy}+o(1)),\beta_{k}(\Sigma^{y}+o(1))\right]

Lemma
D.2
: Induction step showing the following

\displaystyle\left[\tilde{X}^{\prime}_{k+1},\tilde{Z}^{\prime}_{k+1},\tilde{Y}^{\prime}_{k+1}\right]

\displaystyle=\left[\alpha_{k+1}(\Sigma^{x}+o(1)),\beta_{k+1}(\Sigma^{xy}+o(1)),\beta_{k+1}(\Sigma^{y}+o(1))\right]

Proof of Proposition
4.1
.

We will prove the lemma only for
\Gamma^{x}
. The other terms follow in a similar way. From Lemma
D.14
, taking
A_{1}
and
A_{2}
to be all zero matrices we have that:

\displaystyle\hat{b}_{i}(O)=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})|O_{0}=O]

Replacing the above solution in Definition
4.1
we have:

\displaystyle\Gamma^{x}=\mathbb{E}_{O\sim\mu}\left[\left(\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(O_{j})|O_{0}=O]\right)b_{2}(O)^{\top}+b_{2}(O)\left(\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(O_{j})|O_{0}=O]^{\top}\right)-b_{2}(O)b_{2}(O)^{\top}\right]

Since
\{\tilde{O}_{j}\}_{j\geq 0}
comes from Markov chain whose starting distribution is
\mu
, we have:

\displaystyle\Gamma^{x}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})|\tilde{O}_{0}]\right)b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\left(\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})|\tilde{O}_{0}]^{\top}\right)-b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})|\tilde{O}_{0}]b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})|\tilde{O}_{0}]^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}|\tilde{O}_{0}]\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})|\tilde{O}_{0}]^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}|\tilde{O}_{0}]]+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})|\tilde{O}_{0}]^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]

(Fubini-Tonelli Theorem)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})]^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]

(Tower property)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]

∎

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.

We can write recursion (
B.1
) as

\displaystyle y_{k+1}

\displaystyle=y_{k}-\beta_{k}(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+\beta_{k}\left(b_{1}(O_{k})-(A_{11}(O_{k})-A_{11})y_{k}-(A_{12}(O_{k})-A_{12})x_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=y_{k}-\beta_{k}(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+\beta_{k}f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}),

and

\displaystyle x_{k+1}

\displaystyle=x_{k}-\alpha_{k}(A_{21}y_{k}+A_{22}x_{k})+\alpha_{k}\left(b_{2}(O_{k})-(A_{21}(O_{k})-A_{21})y_{k}-(A_{22}(O_{k})-A_{22})x_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=x_{k}-\alpha_{k}(A_{21}y_{k}+A_{22}x_{k})+\alpha_{k}f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}).

We first construct the auxiliary iterates of
\tilde{y}_{k}
and
\tilde{x}_{k}
as follows:

\displaystyle\tilde{y}_{k}

\displaystyle=y_{k}

(C.1)

\displaystyle\tilde{x}_{k}

\displaystyle=L_{k}y_{k}+x_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}y_{k},

(C.2)

where

\displaystyle L_{k}

\displaystyle=0,\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ 0\leq k<k_{L}

(C.3)

\displaystyle L_{k+1}

\displaystyle=(L_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}L_{k}+\beta_{k}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}B_{11}^{k})(I-\beta_{k}B_{11}^{k})^{-1},\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq k_{L},

(C.4)

\displaystyle B_{11}^{k}

\displaystyle=\Delta-A_{12}L_{k}

\displaystyle B_{21}^{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{L_{k}-L_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k}}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\left(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\right)B_{11}^{k}-A_{22}L_{k}

\displaystyle B_{22}^{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\left(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\right)A_{12}+A_{22}=C_{22}^{k}+A_{22},

where we denote
C_{22}^{k}=\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\left(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\right)A_{12}
.
The existence of
k_{L}
is guaranteed due to Lemma
D.17
, the fact that
\Delta
and
A_{12}
are finite, and Assumptions
3.3
on the step size. In addition, this choice of
k_{L}
results in
I\succ\beta_{k}B_{11}^{k}
for all
k\geq k_{L}
.

Then we have the following update for the new variables

\displaystyle\tilde{y}_{k+1}=

\displaystyle\tilde{y}_{k}-\beta_{k}(B_{11}^{k}\tilde{y}_{k}+A_{12}\tilde{x}_{k})+\beta_{k}v_{k}

(C.5)

\displaystyle\tilde{x}_{k+1}=

\displaystyle\tilde{x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}(B_{21}^{k}\tilde{y}_{k}+B_{22}^{k}\tilde{x}_{k})+\alpha_{k}w_{k}+\beta_{k}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k},

(C.6)

where recall
v_{k}=f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})
and
w_{k}=f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})
.

•

Since we assumed
b_{1}=b_{2}=0
, we have
\tilde{y}_{k}=y_{k}=\hat{y}
. By Lemma
D.1
, we get .

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\hat{y}_{k}\hat{y}_{k}^{\top}]=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}+C_{k}^{y}\zeta_{k}^{y}

where
C_{k}^{y}=\tilde{C}_{k}^{y}
and
\|C^{k}_{y}\|\leq c^{*}d^{2}=c^{(y)}d^{2}
.

•

By Lemma
D.1
, we have

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\zeta_{k}^{xy}=\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]=\mathbb{E}[(L_{k}y_{k}+\hat{x}_{k})y_{k}^{\top}]

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\zeta_{k}^{xy}-L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}].

Define
C_{k}^{xy}
such that
\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\zeta_{k}^{xy}-L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]=C_{k}^{xy}\zeta_{k}^{xy}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\|C_{k}^{xy}\|=\bigg\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}-\frac{1}{\zeta_{k}^{xy}}L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\|+\frac{1}{\zeta_{k}^{xy}}\|L_{k}\|\|\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq c^{*}d^{2}+c_{1}^{L}\frac{\beta_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{xy}\alpha_{k}}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}d\beta_{k}+c^{*}d^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}\right)

(Lemma
D.1
and
D.17
)

\displaystyle\leq c^{*}d^{2}+c_{1}^{L}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}d\beta+c^{*}d^{2}\right)=c^{(z)}d^{2}.

where
c^{(z)}=c^{*}+c_{1}^{L}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}\beta+c^{*}\right)
.

•

Again by Lemma
D.1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(L_{k}y_{k}+\hat{x}_{k})(L_{k}y_{k}+\hat{x}_{k})^{\top}]=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}]=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]L_{k}^{\top}-L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]L_{k}^{\top}.

Define
C^{x}_{k}
such that

C^{x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}=\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]L_{k}^{\top}-L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]L_{k}^{\top}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\|C_{k}^{x}\|=\left\|\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}-\frac{1}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\left(L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]L_{k}^{\top}+L_{k}\mathbb{E}[y_{k}\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}]+\mathbb{E}[\hat{x}_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]L_{k}^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\|+\frac{1}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\|L_{k}\|^{2}\|\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{2}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\|L_{k}\|\|\mathbb{E}[y_{k}\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}]\|.

Using Lemma
D.3
, we can bound
\|L_{k}\|\leq\kappa_{Q_{22}}
. For the other terms, we use the previous parts to get,

\displaystyle\|C_{k}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq c^{*}d^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}d\beta_{k}+c^{*}d^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}\right)+\frac{2\kappa_{Q_{22}}}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\left(\sigma^{xy}\tau_{mix}d\beta_{k}+c^{(z)}d^{2}\zeta_{k}^{xy}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c^{*}d^{2}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}d\beta+c^{*}d^{2}\right)+2\kappa_{Q_{22}}\left(\sigma^{xy}\tau_{mix}d\beta+c^{(z)}d^{2}\right)

(
\beta_{k}\leq\beta\zeta_{k}^{x}
)

\displaystyle=c^{(x)}d^{2},

where
c^{(x)}=c^{*}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}\beta+c^{*}\right)+2\kappa_{Q_{22}}\left(\sigma^{xy}\tau_{mix}\beta+c^{(z)}\right)
.

∎

Proof of Proposition
4.2
.

The covariance of
h_{N}
is given as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[h_{N}h_{N}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]+A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ -\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]

Let the first term be denoted as
T_{1}
. In what follows, we will only analyze
T_{1}
and show that

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]=\Gamma^{y}.

Convergence for other terms can be shown by following the exact steps, hence omitted for brevity. Expanding
T_{1}
, we get

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\sum_{k^{\prime}=k+1}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{k^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{k=k^{\prime}+1}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]\right).

Recall that
\{\tilde{O}_{k}\}
is a stationary process. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq 0.

Next, to simplify the second term in
T_{1}
, let
j\in\{1,\dots,N\}
and
k^{\prime}-k=j
. Then, we note that there are exactly
N-j
pairs
(k^{\prime},k)
such that
k^{\prime}-k=j
and
0\leq k<k^{\prime}\leq N-1
. A similar argument holds for the third term, with the only difference that the indices
k
and
k^{\prime}
are swapped. Combining this observation with the strong Markov property, we can rewrite the expression for
T_{1}
as

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]+\frac{1}{N}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}(N-j)\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}(N-j)\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]\right).

To show the convergence of second and third term, we use the mixing property of the Markov chain. Recall that
\{\tilde{O}_{k}\}
is sampled from a finite state ergodic Markov chain, hence it mixes exponentially fast
[
LP17
]
, that is, for all
o\in\mathcal{S}
, we have
d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o)||\mu(\cdot))\leq\rho^{k}
for some
\rho\in[0,1)
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}(N-j)\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}\right]-

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}\right]\Bigg\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}j\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}j\max_{o}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})|\tilde{O}_{0}=o\right]\right\|\left\|\tilde{b}_{1}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}j\max_{o}\left\|\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)\tilde{b}_{1}(o^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\tilde{b}_{1}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}j\max_{o}\left\|\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)-\mu(o^{\prime}))\tilde{b}_{1}(o^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\tilde{b}_{1}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b_{max}^{2}d}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}j\max_{o^{\prime}}d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o^{\prime})||\mu(\cdot))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b_{max}^{2}d}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}j\rho^{k}\leq\frac{b_{max}^{2}d\rho}{N(1-\rho)^{2}}\xrightarrow[N\uparrow\infty]{}0.

Combining the above relations, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}T_{1}=\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right].

Using a similar analysis for
\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})
and the cross terms, we obtain the asymptotic covariance of
h_{N}
as

\displaystyle\lim_{N\to\infty}\mathbb{E}[h_{N}h_{N}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{y}+A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Gamma^{x}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}-\Gamma^{yx}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Gamma^{xy}.

Now, we are only left to show that the r.h.s of the above equation can be equivalently written as
\Gamma^{y}-A_{12}\Sigma^{yx}-\Sigma^{xy}A_{12}^{\top}
. To see this, we first solve for
\Sigma^{xy}
from Eq. (
4.4b
) to get

\displaystyle\Sigma^{xy}=(\Gamma^{xy}-A_{12}\Sigma^{x})A_{22}^{-\top}.

Substituting the above expression in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
4.4c
), we get

\displaystyle\Gamma^{y}-A_{12}\Sigma^{yx}-\Sigma^{xy}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{y}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}(\Gamma^{xy}-A_{12}\Sigma^{x})-(\Gamma^{yx}-\Sigma^{x}A_{12}^{\top})A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{y}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Gamma^{xy}+A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Sigma^{x}A_{12}^{\top}-\Gamma^{yx}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}+A_{12}\Sigma^{x}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{y}+A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}(\Sigma^{x}A_{22}^{\top}+A_{22}\Sigma^{x})A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Gamma^{xy}-\Gamma^{yx}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{y}+A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Gamma^{x}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}\Gamma^{xy}-\Gamma^{yx}A_{22}^{-\top}A_{12}^{\top}

(Eq. (
4.4a
))

∎

Proof for Corollary
4.1.1
.

The claim follows by taking trace on both sides of Eq.
4.1
and using
trace(C_{k}^{y}(0.5))\leq d\|C_{k}^{y}(0.5)\|\leq dc_{0}(0.5)
. Note that since Theorem
4.1
holds for any
\varrho\in(0,1)
, we choose
\varrho=0.5
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
4.4
.

Since in this proposition we are only concerned with convergence,
throughout this proof we replace all the constants with
c
.

From (
D.23
) and (
D.26
) in the proof of Lemma
D.7
, we have

\displaystyle V_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2})V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}c(1+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{c\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(1+V_{k}+W_{k})+\alpha_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}),

\displaystyle W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{2})W_{k}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}c(1+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}V_{k}+\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y})

Let
\omega_{k}=\frac{8\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})\beta_{k}}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta a_{22}\alpha_{k}}
. Define
V_{k}^{\prime}=\omega_{k}V_{k}
. Then, rewriting both the recursions in terms of
V_{k}^{\prime}
we get:

\displaystyle V^{\prime}_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2})V^{\prime}_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}c(\omega_{k}+V^{\prime}_{k}+\omega_{k}W_{k})+\frac{c\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(\omega_{k}+V^{\prime}_{k}+\omega_{k}W_{k})+c\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x})

\displaystyle+c\omega_{k}\frac{1}{k}V^{\prime}_{k}+c\omega_{k}\frac{1}{k}(\alpha_{k}^{2}+\beta_{k})(1+W_{k}),

(by (
D.24
))

\displaystyle W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{2})W_{k}+\frac{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}c}{\omega_{k}}V_{k}^{\prime}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}c(1+W_{k})+\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{4}V^{\prime}_{k}+\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y})

Adding the recursions, we get:

\displaystyle V^{\prime}_{k+1}+W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{4})V^{\prime}_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}c(\omega_{k}+V^{\prime}_{k}+\omega_{k}W_{k})+\frac{c\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(\omega_{k}+V^{\prime}_{k}+\omega_{k}W_{k})+c\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x})

\displaystyle+c\omega_{k}\frac{1}{k}V^{\prime}_{k}+c\omega_{k}\frac{1}{k}(\alpha_{k}^{2}+\beta_{k})(1+W_{k})+(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{2})W_{k}+\frac{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}c}{\omega_{k}}V_{k}^{\prime}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}c(1+W_{k})

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y})

\displaystyle V^{\prime}_{k+1}+W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{8})V^{\prime}_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}c\omega_{k}+\frac{c\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}\omega_{k}+c\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x})+c\omega_{k}\frac{1}{k}(\alpha_{k}^{2}+\beta_{k})

\displaystyle+(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4})W_{k}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}c+\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y})

(for large enough
k
)

\displaystyle V^{\prime}_{k+1}+W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{8})V^{\prime}_{k}+\beta_{k}(\hat{d}_{k}-\hat{d}_{k+1})+(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4})W_{k}+o(\beta_{k})

(
\hat{d}_{k}=c\bar{d}_{k}^{x}+\bar{d}_{k}^{y}
)

\displaystyle V^{\prime}_{k+1}+W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4})(V^{\prime}_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}(\hat{d}_{k}-\hat{d}_{k+1})+o(\beta_{k})

(for large enough
k
)

Let
\bar{K}
be the minimum
k
at the which the above recursion holds. Then opening the recursion from
\bar{K}
to
k
and using the telescopic structure leads to the following:

\displaystyle V^{\prime}_{k}+W_{k}\leq(V^{\prime}_{\bar{K}}+W_{\bar{K}})\prod_{i=\bar{K}}^{k}(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{i}}{4})+\beta_{\bar{K}}\hat{d}_{\bar{K}}\prod_{l=\bar{K}+1}^{k}(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{l}}{4})+\beta_{k}|\hat{d}_{k+1}|+\sum_{j=\bar{K}}^{k}(\beta_{j}^{2}|\hat{d}_{j}|+o(\beta_{k}))\prod_{l=j+1}^{k}(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{l}}{4}).

Notice that for all
j\geq 0
,
\hat{d}_{j}
is upper bounded by a constant due to (
D.24
), (
D.27
) and Lemma
D.7
. Thus, using the observation in
A.1
, we obtain
V_{k}^{\prime}+W_{k}=o(1)
. This shows that
y_{k}\to y^{*}
in mean square sense. To further show that
x_{k}\to x^{*}
, we replace
W_{k}
with
o(1)
in (
D.23
) and expand from
\bar{K}
to
k
to get:

\displaystyle V_{k}\leq V_{\bar{K}}\prod_{i=\bar{K}}^{k}(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{8})+\alpha_{\bar{K}}\bar{d}^{x}_{\bar{K}}\prod_{l=\bar{K}+1}^{k}(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{8})+\alpha_{k}|\bar{d}^{x}_{k+1}|+\sum_{j=\bar{K}}^{k}(\alpha_{j}^{2}|\bar{d}^{x}_{j}|+o(\alpha_{k}))\prod_{l=j+1}^{k}(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{8}).

The claim follows.

∎

Proof of Theorem
4.2
.

In the setting of Polyak-Ruppert averaging, the parameters reduce to the following:

\displaystyle A_{21}(O_{k})=0;\penalty\ b_{1}(O_{k})=0;\penalty\ A_{11}(O_{k})=I;\penalty\ A_{12}(O_{k})=-I:\penalty\ \beta=1

This results in
\Delta=I
. Let
\tilde{b}(\cdot)=b(\cdot)-b+(A-A(\cdot))A^{-1}b
. Then, we have:

\displaystyle\Gamma^{x}=\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}

Note that it is possible to find the explicit expression of
\Sigma^{y}
in the case of Polyak-Ruppert averaging. To show this we have the following three systems of equations:

\displaystyle A\Sigma^{x}+\Sigma^{x}A^{\top}=\Gamma^{x}

\displaystyle-\Sigma^{x}+\Sigma^{xy}A^{\top}=0\Rightarrow\Sigma^{xy}=A^{-\top}\Sigma^{x}

\displaystyle\Sigma^{y}-\Sigma^{yx}-\Sigma^{xy}=0\Rightarrow\Sigma^{y}=\Sigma^{yx}+\Sigma^{xy}

Using second equation in the last one we get:

\displaystyle\Sigma^{y}=\Sigma^{x}A^{-1}+A^{-\top}\Sigma^{x}

Left multiplying
A^{-1}
and right multiplying
A^{-\top}
of the first equation we get:

\displaystyle\Sigma^{x}A^{-\top}+A^{-1}\Sigma^{x}=A^{-1}\Gamma^{x}A^{-\top}

which from the previous equation is equal to
\Sigma^{y}
. Finally, using Theorem
4.1
and replacing
1-\varrho=0.5
defined in
B.4
, we get the result.
∎

Proof for Theorem
4.3
.

Denote the tuple
O_{k}=\{s_{k},a_{k},s_{k+1}\}
and consider the Markov chain
\{O_{l}\}_{l\geq 0}
. Here
\hat{P}(O_{k+1}|O_{k})=\pi_{b}(a_{k+1}|s_{k+1})P(s_{k+2}|s_{k+1},a_{k+1})
and the stationary distribution is given by
\mu(s,a,s^{\prime})=\mu_{b}(s,a)P(s^{\prime}|s,a)
. Since we assume that the behavior policy induces an ergodic Markov chain, we have that
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
satisfies Assumption
3.2
. We will denote
\{\tilde{O}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
as the Markov chain where
\{(s_{0},a_{0})\sim\mu_{b}\}
. Assumption
3.3
is also satisfied, since
\xi=0.75\in(0.5,1)
, and
\beta
is chosen appropriately. Thus, all that is left to verify is that the appropriate matrices in the three settings are Hurwitz. Recall that we defined
A=\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\pi_{b}}}[\rho(s,a)\phi(s)(\phi(s)-\gamma\phi(s^{\prime}))^{\top}]
,
B=\gamma\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\pi_{b}}}[\rho(s,a)\phi(s^{\prime})\phi(s)^{\top}]
,
C=\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\pi_{b}}}[\phi(s)\phi(s)^{\top}]
and
b=\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\pi_{b}}}[\rho(s,a)r(s,a)\phi(s)]
. We verify the Hurwitz property and characterize the variance in the dominant term for each setting as follows:

•

GTD
: Clearly,
A_{22}(O_{k})=I
for all
k\geq 0
in this case which implies
-A_{22}=-I
is Hurwitz. Furthermore,
A_{11}=0
, thus
-\Delta=-A^{\top}A
, which is a positive definite matrix and is therefore Hurwitz.

Next, note that
b_{1}(O_{k})=0
for all
k\geq 0
and
b_{2}(O_{k})=b_{k}
. Let
(\theta^{*},\omega^{*})
denote the fixed point. Then, we define the following:

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{2}(O_{k})=b_{k}-b+(A-A_{k})\theta^{*}.

The above gives us the following asymptotic covariance matrices:

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\omega}=\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]};\penalty\ \penalty\ \Gamma^{\omega\theta}=0;\penalty\ \penalty\ \Gamma^{\theta}=0.

Then, using Theorem
4.1
we get:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{\theta}+\frac{1}{k^{1+(1-\varrho)\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}}C^{\theta}_{k}(\varrho)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{\omega\theta}+\frac{1}{k^{\min(\xi+0.5,2-\xi)}}C^{\omega\theta}_{k}(\varrho)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{\omega}+\frac{1}{k^{\min(1.5\xi,1)}}C^{\omega}_{k}(\varrho)

where
0<\varrho<1
is an arbitrary constant,
\sup_{k}\max\{\|C_{k}^{\omega}(\varrho)\|,\|C_{k}^{\theta\omega}(\varrho)\|,\|C_{k}^{\theta}(\varrho)\|\}<c_{0}(\varrho)<\infty
for some problem dependent constant
c_{0}(\varrho)
, and
\Sigma^{\theta}
,
\Sigma^{\omega\theta}={\Sigma^{\theta\omega}}^{\top}
and
\Sigma^{\omega}
are unique solutions to the following system of equations:

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\omega}=\frac{1}{2}\Gamma^{\omega}

(
A_{22}=I
)

\displaystyle A\Sigma^{\omega}+\Sigma^{\omega\theta}=0

\displaystyle\left(A^{T}A-\frac{1}{2\beta}I\right)\Sigma^{\theta}+\Sigma^{\theta}\left(A^{T}A-\frac{1}{2\beta}I\right)=-A^{\top}\Sigma^{\theta\omega}-\Sigma^{\omega\theta}A.

The variance
\sigma^{2}_{GTD}
is obtained by taking the trace of
\Sigma^{\theta}
.

•

GTD2
: In this setting
A_{22}(O_{k})=C_{k}
. Thus, we have
-A_{22}=-C
. Since
\Phi
is a full-rank matrix, we have that
-C
is a Hurwitz matrix as
-x^{\top}Cx=-\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{b}}[x^{\top}\phi(s)\phi(s)^{\top}x]<0
, in particular it is negative definite. However, similar to GTD,
A_{11}=0
in this case, so
-\Delta=-A^{\top}C^{-1}A
which is negative definite and thus Hurwitz.

Furthermore, we again have
b_{1}(O_{k})=0
for all
k\geq 0
and
b_{2}(O_{k})=b_{k}
. However, the definition of
b_{2}(\cdot)
will change as
A_{22}(O_{k})\neq A
unlike the previous setting.

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{2}(O_{k})=b_{k}-b+(A-A_{k})\theta^{*}+(C-C_{k})\omega^{*}.

Thus, we have the following asymptotic covariance matrices:

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\omega}=\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]};\penalty\ \penalty\ \Gamma^{\omega\theta}=0;\penalty\ \penalty\ \Gamma^{\theta}=0.

Then, again using Theorem
4.1
we get:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{\theta}+\frac{1}{k^{1+(1-\varrho)\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}}C^{\theta}_{k}(\varrho)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{\omega\theta}+\frac{1}{k^{\min(\xi+0.5,2-\xi)}}C^{\omega\theta}_{k}(\varrho)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{\omega}+\frac{1}{k^{\min(1.5\xi,1)}}C^{\omega}_{k}(\varrho)

where
0<\varrho<1
is an arbitrary constant,
\sup_{k}\max\{\|C_{k}^{\omega}(\varrho)\|,\|C_{k}^{\theta\omega}(\varrho)\|,\|C_{k}^{\theta}(\varrho)\|\}<c_{0}(\varrho)<\infty
for some problem dependent constant
c_{0}(\varrho)
, and
\Sigma^{\theta}
,
\Sigma^{\omega\theta}={\Sigma^{\theta\omega}}^{\top}
and
\Sigma^{\omega}
are unique solutions to the following system of equations:

\displaystyle C\Sigma^{\omega}+\Sigma^{\omega}C^{\top}=\Gamma^{\omega}

(
C^{\top}=C
)

\displaystyle A\Sigma^{\omega}+\Sigma^{\omega\theta}C=0

\displaystyle\left(A^{T}C^{-1}A-\frac{1}{2\beta}I\right)\Sigma^{\theta}+\Sigma^{\theta}\left(A^{T}C^{-1}A-\frac{1}{2\beta}I\right)=-A^{\top}\Sigma^{\theta\omega}-\Sigma^{\omega\theta}A.

The variance
\sigma^{2}_{GTD2}
is obtained by taking the trace of
\Sigma^{\theta}
.

•

TDC
: Note that
A=C-B^{\top}=C^{\top}-B^{\top}
. Thus we have,

\displaystyle A-BC^{-1}A

\displaystyle=(C-B)C^{-1}A

\displaystyle=A^{\top}C^{-1}A

Since
x^{\top}A^{\top}C^{-1}Ax>0
,
-(A-BC^{-1}A)
is Hurwitz. Let
(\theta^{*},\omega^{*})
denote the fixed point. Note that unlike previous cases,
b_{1}(O_{k})\neq 0
. Thus, we define the following:

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{1}(O_{k})=b_{k}-b+(A-A_{k})\theta^{*}+(B-B_{k})\omega^{*}

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{2}(O_{k})=b_{k}-b+(A-A_{k})\theta^{*}+(C-C_{k})\omega^{*}

Then, we have the following asymptotic covariance matrices:

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\omega}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\omega\theta}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\theta}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})\tilde{b}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}.

Then, employing Theorem
4.1
we get:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{\theta}+\frac{1}{k^{1+(1-\varrho)\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}}C^{\theta}_{k}(\varrho)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\theta_{k}-\theta^{*})(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{\omega\theta}+\frac{1}{k^{\min(\xi+0.5,2-\xi)}}C^{\omega\theta}_{k}(\varrho)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})(\omega_{k}-\omega^{*})^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{\omega}+\frac{1}{k^{\min(1.5\xi,1)}}C^{\omega}_{k}(\varrho)

where
0<\varrho<1
is an arbitrary constant,
\sup_{k}\max\{\|C_{k}^{\omega}(\varrho)\|,\|C_{k}^{\theta\omega}(\varrho)\|,\|C_{k}^{\theta}(\varrho)\|\}<c_{0}(\varrho)<\infty
for some problem dependent constant
c_{0}(\varrho)
, and
\Sigma^{\theta}
,
\Sigma^{\omega\theta}={\Sigma^{\theta\omega}}^{\top}
and
\Sigma^{\omega}
are unique solutions to the following system of equations:

\displaystyle C\Sigma^{\omega}+\Sigma^{\omega}C^{\top}=\Gamma^{\omega}

\displaystyle B\Sigma^{\omega}+\Sigma^{\omega\theta}C^{\top}=\Gamma^{\omega\theta}

\displaystyle\left(A^{T}C^{-1}A-\frac{1}{2\beta}I\right)\Sigma^{\theta}+\Sigma^{\theta}\left(A^{T}C^{-1}A-\frac{1}{2\beta}I\right)=\Gamma^{\theta}-B\Sigma^{\theta\omega}-\Sigma^{\omega\theta}B^{\top}

The variance
\sigma^{2}_{TDC}
is obtained by taking the trace of
\Sigma^{\theta}
.

∎

Proof of Proposition
4.3
.

1.

\mathcal{B}\subsetneq\mathcal{A}
: By definition, it is clear that
\mathcal{B}\subseteq\mathcal{A}
. Next, consider the following matrix

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}A_{11}=-4&A_{12}=-2\\
A_{21}=-1&A_{22}=-3\end{bmatrix}.

Here we have
A\in\mathcal{A}
. Furthermore, there does not exist any
\kappa>0
such that
-A_{\kappa}
is Hurwitz, which means that
A\notin\mathcal{B}
. This can be easily seen by observing that sum of the eigenvalues is equal to trace of the matrix and the
tr(-A_{\kappa})=3\kappa+4>0
. Thus, the
-A_{\kappa}
cannot be Hurwitz for any
\kappa>0
.

2.

\mathcal{C}\cup\mathcal{D}\subsetneq\mathcal{B}
:
Firstly, by definition, it is easy to see that
\mathcal{C}\subset\mathcal{B}
. Secondly, by
[
CBD24
, Theorem 6]
, we have
\mathcal{D}\subset\mathcal{B}
. Next, we show that
\mathcal{B}\backslash(\mathcal{C}\cup\mathcal{D})\neq\varnothing
. Consider the following matrix:

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}A_{11}=2&A_{12}=-4\\
A_{21}=3&A_{22}=-5\end{bmatrix}.

Since
tr(-A)=3>0
,
-A
is not Hurwitz, and hence
A\notin\mathcal{C}
. In addition,
-A_{22}=5>0
, which means that
A\notin\mathcal{D}
.
Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle A_{0.2}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}2&-4\\
0.6&-1\end{bmatrix}.

Then, the eigenvalues of
-A_{0.2}
are
-0.5\pm i\sqrt{15}/10
. Hence,
-A_{0.2}
is Hurwitz, and
A\in\mathcal{B}
.

3.

\mathcal{C}\backslash\mathcal{D}\neq\varnothing
: Consider the following matrix

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}A_{11}=3&A_{12}=4\\
A_{21}=-1&A_{22}=-1\end{bmatrix}

Both the eigenvalues of
-A
are
=-1
, which shows that
A\subset\mathcal{C}
. However,
-A_{22}=1>0
, which means that
A\notin\mathcal{D}
.

4.

\mathcal{D}\backslash\mathcal{C}\neq\varnothing
:
Consider the following matrix

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}A_{11}=-5&A_{12}=3\\
A_{21}=-4&A_{22}=2\end{bmatrix}.

Then,
-A_{22}=-2<0
and
-\Delta=-(-5-3\times 0.5\times-4)=-1<0
. Hence,
A\in\mathcal{D}
. However,
tr(-A)=3>0
, which means that
A
is not Hurwitz, and hence
A\notin\mathcal{C}
.

5.

\mathcal{C}\cap\mathcal{D}\neq\varnothing
: Consider the following matrix

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}A_{11}=4&A_{12}=2\\
A_{21}=1&A_{22}=3\end{bmatrix}

The eigenvalues of
-A
are
-2
and
-5
. Hence,
A\in\mathcal{C}
. In addition,
-A_{22}=-3<0
and
-\Delta=-(4-2\cdot\frac{1}{3}\cdot 1)=-\frac{10}{3}<0
. Hence,
A\in\mathcal{D}
.

∎

Appendix D
Lemmas

Technical lemmas

Lemma D.1
.

Suppose that Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
, and
3.3
are satisfied.
For the iterations of
\tilde{x}_{k}
and
\tilde{y}_{k}
in (
C.5
) and (
C.6
) we have

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}

(D.1)

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k}

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\zeta_{k}^{xy}

(D.2)

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}_{k}

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}+\tilde{C}_{k}^{y}\zeta_{k}^{y},

(D.3)

where
\Sigma^{x}
,
\Sigma^{xy}
and
\Sigma^{y}
are defined in (
4.4a
), (
4.4b
), and (
4.4c
), and
\sup_{k}\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\|,\|\tilde{C}^{xy}_{k}\|,\|\tilde{C}^{y}_{k}\|\}\leq c^{*}d^{2}<\infty
for some problem dependent constant
c^{*}
.

Lemma D.2
.

Suppose that Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
, and
3.3
are satisfied. For
k\geq 0
, the iterations of
\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}
,
\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}
, and
\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}
satisfy

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}

(D.4)

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}

(D.5)

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}+\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y},

(D.6)

where
\Sigma^{x}
,
\Sigma^{xy}
and
\Sigma^{y}
are defined in (
4.4a
), (
4.4b
), and (
4.4c
), and
\sup_{k}\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},1\}\leq\bar{c}d^{2}<\infty
for some problem-dependent constant
\bar{c}
.

Proof of technical lemmas

Proof of Lemma
D.1
.

We first focus on
\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}
. Recall that
\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}=\tilde{X}_{k}+\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})
. Using Lemma
D.8
, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{X}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}\|+2\alpha_{k}\|d_{k}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}\|+\frac{4\alpha_{k}\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}\|+\frac{4d\alpha_{k}\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)

(Lemma
D.7
)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}d\left(\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\right)+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\bar{c}d^{2}\zeta_{k}^{x}.

(Lemma
D.2
)

Since
\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]\leq d\|\tilde{X}_{k}\|
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}\leq d\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\left(\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\right)}+d^{1.5}\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}\bar{c}\zeta_{k}^{x}}.

Define
\tilde{C}_{k}^{x}=\tilde{C}_{k}^{\prime x}-\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}(d_{k}^{x}+d_{k}^{x\top})
. Using Lemma
D.8
and the above bound on
\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]
, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{\prime x}\|+\frac{2\alpha_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\|d_{k}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\left(d^{1.5}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\left(\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\right)}+d^{2}\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}\bar{c}\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right).

Recall that by definition
\alpha_{k}^{1.5}\leq\alpha^{1.5}\zeta_{k}^{x}
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{4\sqrt{3}d^{2}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\left(\alpha^{1.5}\sqrt{\left(\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\right)}+\alpha\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}\bar{c}}\right)=c^{*(x)}d^{2}.

Next, recall that
\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}=\tilde{Y}_{k}+\beta_{k}(d_{k}^{yv}+d_{k}^{yv\top})
. By following the exact set of arguments as before, one can show that

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}\leq d\sqrt{\beta_{k}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\right)}+d^{1.5}\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\bar{c}\zeta_{k}^{y}}.

Define
\tilde{C}_{k}^{y}=\tilde{C}_{k}^{\prime y}-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{y}}(d_{k}^{yv}+d_{k}^{yv\top})
. Using Lemma
D.8
and the above bound on
\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]
, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{y}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{\prime y}\|+\frac{2\beta_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\|d_{k}^{yv}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(d^{1.5}\sqrt{\beta_{k}\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\right)}+d^{2}\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\bar{c}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right).

Again by definition
\beta_{k}^{1.5}\leq\beta^{1.5}\zeta_{k}^{y}
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{y}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{4\sqrt{3}d^{2}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(\beta^{1.5}\sqrt{\left(\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\right)}+\beta\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\bar{c}}\right)=c^{*(y)}d^{2}.

Finally, from the definition of
\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k}=

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}-(\alpha_{k}d_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}d_{k}^{xv\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}-(\alpha_{k}d_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}d_{k}^{xv\top}).

(by Lemma
D.2
)

Define
\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}=\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}-\frac{\alpha_{k}d_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}d_{k}^{xv\top}}{\zeta_{k}^{xy}}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\|\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{k}\|d_{k}^{y}\|+\beta_{k}\|d_{k}^{xv}\|}{\zeta_{k}^{xy}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\zeta^{xy}_{k}}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\zeta^{xy}_{k}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}\right).

(Lemma
D.8
)

Recall from the previous parts, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq d\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}}+d^{1.5}\sqrt{c^{*(x)}\zeta_{k}^{x}}

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq d\sqrt{\beta_{k}\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}}+d^{1.5}\sqrt{c^{*(y)}\zeta_{k}^{y}}.

Plugging the above relation in the bound for
\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\|
, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}_{k}^{xy}\|\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}d^{2}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\zeta^{xy}_{k}}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\left(\sqrt{\beta_{k}\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}}+\sqrt{c^{*(y)}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\zeta^{xy}_{k}}\left(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}}+\sqrt{c^{*(x)}\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{22}}}\bar{c}d^{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}d^{2}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(\left(\alpha+\beta\varrho_{x}\right)\left(\sqrt{\beta\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}}+\sqrt{c^{*(y)}}\right)+\beta\left(\sqrt{\alpha\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}}+\sqrt{c^{*(x)}}\right)\right)=c^{*(z)}d^{2}.

Thus, we have
\sup_{k}\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{x}_{k}\|,\|\tilde{C}^{xy}_{k}\|,\|\tilde{C}^{y}_{k}\|\}\leq c^{*}d^{2}
, where
c^{*}=\max\{c^{*(x)},c^{*(z)},c^{*(y)}\}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
D.2
.

For consistency, throughout the proof
R_{k}^{(\cdot)}
represents remainder or higher order terms. Furthermore, note that by equivalence of norms
\|\cdot\|\leq\kappa_{Q_{22}}\|\cdot\|_{Q_{22}}
and
\|\cdot\|\leq\kappa_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\cdot\|_{Q_{\Delta}}
which will be used extensively without explicitly mentioning.

We prove this lemma by induction. Assume that at time
k
, we have the following decomposition of the terms.

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}

(D.7)

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{xy}+\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}

(D.8)

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}+\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y},

(D.9)

where
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\}=\hbar_{k}
. Note that
\hbar_{k}
depends on
k
.

The goal of this proof is to show that there exists a problem dependent constant
k_{0}
such that for
k\geq k_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}\}\leq\max\left\{\hbar_{k},\hat{c}\right\},

where
\hat{c}
is a problem dependent constant, independent of
\hbar_{k}
or
k
. We show that this constant
k_{0}
is given as the maximum of six problem-dependent constants
k_{1},\bar{k}_{1},\bar{k}_{2},\bar{k}_{3},\bar{k}_{4},\bar{k}_{5}
. The constant
k_{1}
was defined in the proof of Lemma
D.3
, and the rest of the constants are defined in Eq. (
D.10
)-(
D.14
) in the proof. Finding the closed form expressions of these constants will lead to the proof being extremely messy. Hence, we will only highlight the conditions that they must satisfy. It is worth noting that if
K_{0}
in the step-size is chosen large enough, then
k_{0}
can be set to zero. Having this, we define

\displaystyle\bar{c}=\max\left\{\max_{1\leq k\leq k_{0}}\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\},\hat{c}\right\}.

for a problem-dependent constant
\bar{c}
. Then by induction, we have that
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\}\leq\bar{c}
for all
k\geq 0.
.

1.

For
k\geq k_{1}
, by the definition of
L_{k}
in (
C.4
), we have
B_{21}^{k}=0
. We have

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k+1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k+1}\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[((I-\alpha_{k}B_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k})((I-\alpha_{k}B_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k})^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[((I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k})((I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k})^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{22}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}^{2}A_{22}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{22}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\alpha_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}(C_{22}^{k})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})^{\top}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}^{2}u_{k}u_{k}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}u_{k}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{22}^{\top}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\alpha_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}(C_{22}^{k})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})^{\top}+\alpha_{k}^{2}A_{22}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{22}^{\top}}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}u_{k}^{\top}]}_{T_{2}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}\left((I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}u_{k}^{\top}]+\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}](I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})^{\top}\right)}_{T_{3}}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k+1}(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})-\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}^{2}A_{22}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})+\alpha_{k}^{2}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})A_{22}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}^{3}A_{22}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})A_{22}^{\top}}_{T_{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}^{2}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})(C_{22}^{k})^{\top}+\alpha_{k}^{2}C_{22}^{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})^{\top}}_{T_{5}}

For
T_{1}
, from Definition
B.5
and Lemma
D.3
, we have
\|C_{22}^{k}\|\leq\varrho_{x}A_{max}\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}
. By the assumption of induction, we have
\|\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}\|\leq\|\Sigma^{x}\|\alpha_{k}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}
. Furtheremore, note that
\|I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}\|\leq 1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max}
.
In addition, by Lemma
D.16
and
D.15
we have

\displaystyle\|\Sigma^{x}\|\leq\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}.

Hence, we have:

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\|(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}{C_{22}^{k}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})(\alpha_{k}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x})\varrho_{x}A_{max}

\displaystyle=\bar{c}_{1}d\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}+\bar{c}_{2}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x},

where
\bar{c}_{1}=(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\tau_{mix}\sigma^{x}\varrho_{x}A_{max}
and
\bar{c}_{2}=(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\kappa_{Q_{22}}\varrho_{x}A_{max}

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\|-C_{22}^{k}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}\varrho_{x}A_{max}(\alpha_{k}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x})(1+\alpha A_{max})

\displaystyle=\bar{c}_{3}d\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}+\bar{c}_{4}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}

where
\bar{c}_{3}=\varrho_{x}A_{max}(1+\alpha A_{max})\tau_{mix}\sigma^{x}
and
\bar{c}_{4}=\varrho_{x}A_{max}(1+\alpha A_{max})\kappa_{Q_{22}}
. In addition,

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\|A_{22}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{22}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq A_{max}^{2}(\tau_{mix}\sigma^{x}d\alpha_{k}^{3}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}\alpha_{k}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq A_{max}^{2}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta}(\tau_{mix}\sigma^{x}d\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}\beta_{k})

(
\xi>0.5\implies\alpha_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta}\beta_{k}
)

Combining all the bounds together, we get

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\|T_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\bar{c}_{5}d\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}+\bar{c}_{6}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x},

where
\bar{c}_{5}=\bar{c}_{1}+\bar{c}_{3}+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta}A_{max}^{2}\tau_{mix}\sigma^{x}
and
\bar{c}_{6}=\bar{c}_{2}+\bar{c}_{4}+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta}A_{max}^{2}\kappa_{Q_{22}}
.

For
T_{2}
, using Lemma
D.12
, we have

\displaystyle T_{2}=\alpha_{k}^{2}\Gamma_{22}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\check{R}^{u}_{k}

where
\|\check{R}^{u}_{k}\|\leq\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)^{2}\left(\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right)+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\varrho_{x}\left(\|\Gamma_{21}\|+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\|\Gamma_{11}\|\right)
.

For
T_{3}
, we first study
\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]
. We have
\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]=\mathbb{E}[w_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\mathbb{E}[v_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]
. By Lemma
D.13
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{xw}-d_{k+1}^{xw}+G^{(2,2)}_{k}

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\left[\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv}+G^{(1,2)}_{k}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{xw}-d_{k+1}^{xw}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\left(d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv}\right)+R_{k}^{(1)},

where
\|R_{k}^{(1)}\|\leq g_{3}d^{2}(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x})(\alpha_{k}^{1.5}+\beta_{k})+\frac{b_{max}^{2}d\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}\beta_{k}+\hbar_{k}g_{4}d\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x})
. Recall that
d_{k}^{x}=d_{k}^{xw}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})d_{k}^{xv}
. Thus, we can rewrite the above expression as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{x}-d_{k+1}^{x}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}(L_{k+2}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\right)d_{k+1}^{xv}+R_{k}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{x}-d_{k+1}^{x}+R_{k}^{(2)},

where
\|R_{k}^{(2)}\|\leq\|R_{k}^{(1)}\|+\left\|\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}(L_{k+2}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\right)d_{k+1}^{xv}\right\|
. Observe we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}(L_{k+2}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\right)d_{k+1}^{xv}=

\displaystyle\Bigg(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}(L_{k+2}-L_{k+1})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\right)(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\Bigg)d_{k+1}^{xv}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\|d^{xv}_{k+1}\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}]}

(Lemma
D.8
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}d.

(Lemma
D.7
)

By Lemma,
D.3
we have

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}\|L_{k+2}-L_{k+1}\|\leq c^{L}_{2}\beta_{k+1}.

And by Lemma,
D.18
we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\right)(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\leq\frac{\varrho_{x}(1-\xi)}{\alpha}\beta_{k}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}(L_{k+2}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})-\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\right)d_{k+1}^{xv}\right\|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(2c^{L}_{2}+\frac{\varrho_{x}(1-\xi)}{\alpha}\right)d\beta_{k}.

(
\beta_{k+1}\leq 2\beta_{k}
)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\|R_{k}^{(2)}\|

\displaystyle\leq g_{3}d^{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)(\alpha_{k}^{1.5}+\beta_{k})+\left(\frac{b_{max}^{2}\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}+\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(2c^{L}_{2}+\frac{\varrho_{x}(1-\xi)}{\alpha}\right)\right)d\beta_{k}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\hbar_{k}g_{4}d\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{3}=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+d_{k}^{x\top}-d_{k+1}^{x}-d_{k+1}^{x\top})+\alpha_{k}^{2}\left[\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}+b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}\right]+R_{k}^{(3)},

.

\displaystyle\|R_{k}^{(3)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}\|R_{k}^{(2)}\|+2A_{max}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}u_{k}^{\top}]\|.

To bound the second term, we proceed as follows:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}u_{k}^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|u_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}\sqrt{6d\left(1+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)}

(Lemma
D.9
)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{1}d^{2}+\hbar_{k}d\kappa_{Q_{22}}\zeta_{k}^{x}}\sqrt{6d\left(1+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)}

(Lemma
D.20
and Lemma
D.10
)

Combining both the bounds together, we get

\displaystyle\|R_{k}^{(3)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\bar{c}_{7}d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{2.5}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k})+\bar{c}_{8}\hbar_{k}d\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}.

where

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{7}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigg\{\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\left(g_{3}+2A_{max}\sqrt{6\underline{c}_{1}\left(1+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)}\right)\,

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \left(\frac{b_{max}^{2}\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}+\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(2c^{L}_{2}+\frac{\varrho_{x}(1-\xi)}{\alpha}\right)\right)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{8}

\displaystyle=\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\left(g_{4}+2A_{max}\sqrt{6\kappa_{Q_{22}}\left(1+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)}\right).

For

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{10}

\displaystyle=A_{max}(2+\alpha A_{max})\frac{2\sqrt{3\kappa_{Q_{22}}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right).

For

\displaystyle\|T_{5}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\|d_{k}^{x}\|\varrho_{x}A_{max}

)

where
\bar{c}_{11}=\frac{8\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\varrho_{x}A_{max}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)
.

Hence, we have the following recursion

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k+1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{22}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\Gamma^{x}+(\alpha_{k+1}-\alpha_{k})(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})+R_{k}^{(4)}

where
\|R_{k}^{(4)}\|\leq\bar{c}_{12}d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{2.5}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k})+\bar{c}_{13}\hbar_{k}d(\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}})
. Here

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{12}

\displaystyle=\bar{c}_{5}+\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)^{2}\check{c}_{1}+\varrho_{x}\left(\|\Gamma_{21}\|+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\|\Gamma_{11}\|\right)+\bar{c}_{7}+\bar{c}_{9}+\bar{c}_{11},

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{13}

\displaystyle=\bar{c}_{6}+\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)^{2}\check{c}_{2}+\bar{c}_{8}+\bar{c}_{10}.

Furthermore, to bound
(\alpha_{k+1}-\alpha_{k})(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})
, we proceed as follows:

\displaystyle\|(\alpha_{k+1}-\alpha_{k})(d_{k+1}^{x}+{d_{k+1}^{x}}^{\top})\|\leq

\displaystyle 2|\alpha_{k+1}-\alpha_{k}|\|d_{k+1}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\xi}{\beta}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\|d_{k+1}^{x}\|

(Lemma
D.18
and Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\xi}{\beta}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}]}

(Lemma
D.9
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\xi d}{\beta}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}

(Lemma
D.7
)

Hence,

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k+1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{22}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\Gamma^{x}+R_{k}^{(5)},

where
\|R_{k}^{(5)}\|\leq\left(\bar{c}_{12}+\frac{\xi}{\beta}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}\right)d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{2.5}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k})+\bar{c}_{13}\hbar_{k}d(\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}})
.

By definition of
\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k+1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}(\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x})-\alpha_{k}(\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x})A_{22}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\Gamma^{x}+R_{k}^{(5)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k+1}\Sigma^{x}+(\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k+1})\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}A_{22}^{\top}+R_{k}^{(5)}.

(Eq. (
4.4a
))

Define
\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\zeta_{k+1}^{x}=(\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k+1})\Sigma^{x}+\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}A_{22}^{\top}+R_{k}^{5}
. We have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\underbrace{\frac{|\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k+1}|}{\zeta_{k+1}^{x}}\|\Sigma^{x}\|_{Q_{22}}}_{T_{6}}+\underbrace{\frac{\zeta_{k}^{x}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{x}}\left\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}A_{22}^{\top}\right\|_{Q_{22}}}_{T_{7}}+\frac{1}{\zeta_{k+1}^{x}}\|R_{k}^{5}\|_{Q_{22}}.

For
T_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{6}\leq

\displaystyle\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}}{\beta\zeta_{k}^{x}}

(Lemma
D.18
and Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}\xi\alpha}{(k+K_{0})^{1+\xi-\min(1.5\xi,1)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}\xi\alpha_{k}.

(
1-\min(1.5\xi,1)\geq 0
)

For
T_{7}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{7}=

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}A_{22}^{\top}\right\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}A_{22}^{\top}\right\|_{Q_{22}}\left(\frac{\zeta_{k}^{x}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{x}}-1\right).

But we have
\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}A_{22}^{\top}=(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}^{2}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}A_{22}^{\top}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}-\alpha_{k}\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}A_{22}^{\top}\right\|_{Q_{22}}\leq

\displaystyle\|I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

(Lemma
D.21
)

Note that for the last inequality we assume that
k\geq k_{C}
. Combining the bounds together and using Lemma
D.18
for the second term, we have

\displaystyle T_{7}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}++\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{2}{k+K_{0}}\left((1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\frac{2(1+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha^{2})}{k+K_{0}}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\frac{2+3\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{k+K_{0}}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}.

(
\xi>0.5
)

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}\xi\alpha_{k}+\frac{2+3\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{k+K_{0}}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{1}{\zeta_{k+1}^{x}}\left(\left(\bar{c}_{12}+\frac{\xi}{\beta}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}\right)d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{2.5}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k})+\bar{c}_{13}\hbar_{k}d(\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}})\right).

Observe that
\zeta_{k}^{x}=\frac{1}{(k+K_{0})^{\min\{1.5\xi,1\}}}
. Thus,
\frac{\alpha_{k}^{2.5}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{x}}\leq 2(\alpha^{1.5}+\beta)\alpha_{k}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}\xi\alpha_{k}+\frac{2+3\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{k+K_{0}}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +2\left(\bar{c}_{12}+\frac{\xi}{\beta}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}\right)d^{2}(\alpha^{1.5}+\beta)\alpha_{k}+2\bar{c}_{13}\hbar_{k}d\left(\beta_{k}+\frac{\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\hbar_{k}+\left(\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}d\sigma^{x}\xi+2\left(\bar{c}_{12}+\frac{\xi}{\beta}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}\right)d^{2}(\alpha^{1.5}+\beta)\right)\alpha_{k}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{2+3\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{k+K_{0}}\hbar_{k}+2\bar{c}_{13}\hbar_{k}d\left(\beta_{k}+\frac{\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}}\right).

(
\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\hbar_{k}
)

Let
\bar{k}_{1}
be a large enough constant such that

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{k}a_{22}}{2}\geq\frac{2+3\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}A_{max}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{k+K_{0}}+2\bar{c}_{13}d\left(\beta_{k}+\frac{\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}}\right)\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq\bar{k}_{1}

(D.10)

Furthermore, define
\bar{c}^{(x)}=\kappa_{Q_{22}}\tau_{mix}\sigma^{x}\xi+2\left(\bar{c}_{12}+\frac{\xi}{\beta}\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}\right)(\alpha^{1.5}+\beta)
. Then, for
k\geq\max\{k_{1},\bar{k}_{1}\}

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{k}a_{22}}{2}\right)\hbar_{k}+\bar{c}^{(x)}d^{2}\alpha_{k}.

Hence, we have
\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\max\left\{\hbar_{k},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(x)}d^{2}}{a_{22}}\right\}
.

2.

For
\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}
, we proceed as follows:

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k+1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k+1}\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}d_{k+1}^{y}+\beta_{k+1}{d_{k+1}^{xv}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[((I-\alpha_{k}B_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k})((I-\beta_{k}B_{11}^{k})\tilde{y}_{k}-\beta_{k}A_{12}\tilde{x}_{k}+\beta_{k}v_{k})^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}d_{k+1}^{y}+\beta_{k+1}{d_{k+1}^{xv}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[((I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k})((I-\beta_{k}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k}))\tilde{y}_{k}-\beta_{k}A_{12}\tilde{x}_{k}+\beta_{k}v_{k})^{\top}]

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k+1}d_{k+1}^{y}+\beta_{k+1}{d_{k+1}^{xv}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}(I-\beta_{k}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k}))^{\top}-\beta_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}v_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}u_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}(I-\beta_{k}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k}))^{\top}-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{12}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}u_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]+\alpha_{k+1}d_{k+1}^{y}+\beta_{k+1}{d_{k+1}^{xv}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}-\beta_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\beta_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(A_{22}^{\top}+C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{12}^{\top}+\beta_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}v_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}u_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}u_{k}v_{k}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(A_{22}+C_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}v_{k}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}u_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}A_{12}^{\top}]

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k+1}d_{k+1}^{y}+\beta_{k+1}{d_{k+1}^{xv}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}-\beta_{k}\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{12}^{\top}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\beta_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}{(\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime})}^{\top}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(A_{22}^{\top}+C_{22}^{k})\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{12}^{\top}}_{T_{8}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]+\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]}_{T_{9}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(A_{22}+C_{22}^{k})\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}](\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})^{\top}-\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]A_{12}^{\top}}_{T_{10}}

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{r}+\beta_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})(\alpha_{k}d_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}{d_{k}^{xv}}^{\top})(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})^{\top}+\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}(\alpha_{k}{d_{k}^{y}}^{\top}+\beta_{k}{d_{k}^{xv}})\\
-\alpha_{k}^{2}\beta_{k}(A_{22}^{\top}+C_{22}^{k})(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})A_{12}^{\top}\end{array}\right\}T_{11}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+\alpha_{k}A_{22}(\alpha_{k}d_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}{d_{k}^{xv}}^{\top})+\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})A_{12}^{\top}}_{T_{12}}+\alpha_{k+1}d_{k+1}^{y}+\beta_{k+1}{d_{k+1}^{xv}}^{\top}-\alpha_{k}d_{k}^{y}-\beta_{k}{d_{k}^{xv}}^{\top}

•

For
T_{8}
, we have:

\displaystyle T_{8}=\underbrace{-\beta_{k}(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})^{\top}}_{T_{8,1}}\underbrace{-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k}(\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime})^{\top}}_{T_{8,2}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(A_{22}^{\top}+C_{22}^{k})\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}A_{12}^{\top}}_{T_{8,3}}.

By assumptions on induction, we get:

\displaystyle\|T_{8,1}\|\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\|(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}-\alpha_{k}C_{22}^{k})\|\|\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}\|\|(\Delta-A_{12}L_{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\varrho_{y}(\beta_{k}\sigma^{xy}d\tau_{mix}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy})

(Eq. (
D.8
), Lemmas
D.16
and
D.15
)

Recall
\|C_{22}^{k}\|\leq\varrho_{x}A_{max}\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}
from Definition
B.5
and Lemma
D.3
, we have:

\displaystyle\|T_{8,2}\|\leq\varrho_{x}A_{max}\beta_{k}(\beta_{k}\sigma^{xy}d\tau_{mix}+\kappa_{Q_{22}}\hbar_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}).

In addition, we have:

\displaystyle\|T_{8,3}\|\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\|(A_{22}^{\top}+C_{22}^{k})\|\|\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}\|\|A_{12}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}A_{max}^{2}\left(1+\varrho_{x}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)(\sigma^{x}d\tau_{mix}\alpha_{k}+\hbar_{k}\kappa_{Q_{22}}\zeta_{k}^{x})

Combining all the bounds, we have:

\displaystyle\|T_{8}\|\leq\bar{c}_{14}d\beta_{k}^{2}+\bar{c}_{15}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy},

where
\bar{c}_{14}=(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\varrho_{y}\sigma^{xy}\tau_{mix}+\varrho_{x}A_{max}\sigma^{xy}\tau_{mix}+\alpha^{2}A_{max}^{2}\left(1+\varrho_{x}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}/\beta
and
\bar{c}_{15}=(1+\alpha A_{max}+\beta\varrho_{x}A_{max})\varrho_{y}\kappa_{Q_{22}}+\varrho_{x}A_{max}\kappa_{Q_{22}}+\alpha A_{max}^{2}\left(1+\varrho_{x}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\kappa_{Q_{22}}
. Here we used the fact that
\alpha_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}\leq\alpha\zeta_{k}^{xy}
.

•

For
T_{9}
, we have:

\displaystyle T_{9}=\underbrace{\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{x}_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]}_{T_{9,1}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]}_{T_{9,2}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[u_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]}_{T_{9,3}}.

For
T_{9,1}
, by Lemma
D.13
we have

\displaystyle T_{9,1}=\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})^{\top}]}+\beta_{k}(d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv})^{\top}+\beta_{k}{G_{k}^{(1,2)}}^{\top}.

For
T_{9,2}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{9,2}=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{k}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[w_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]+\beta_{k}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\mathbb{E}\left[v_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\left(\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{yw}-d_{k+1}^{yw}+G^{(2,1)}_{k}\right)

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\left(\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{yv}-d_{k+1}^{yv}+G^{(1,1)}_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\alpha_{k}\left(d_{k}^{yw}-d_{k+1}^{yw}\right)+\beta_{k}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})(d_{k}^{yv}-d_{k+1}^{yv})+R_{k}^{(6)},

where

\displaystyle\|R_{k}^{(6)}\|\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\|G^{(2,1)}_{k}\|+\beta_{k}\|L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|\left(\beta_{k}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}\right\|+\|G_{k}^{(1,1)}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\left(g_{1}d^{2}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+g_{2}d\hbar_{k}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)+\beta_{k}\varrho_{x}\left(\beta_{k}\frac{b_{max}^{2}d}{1-\rho}+g_{1}d^{2}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+g_{2}d\hbar_{k}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{16}d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\beta_{k}^{2})+\bar{c}_{17}d\hbar_{k}\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}},

where
\bar{c}_{16}=g_{1}\left(1+\varrho_{x}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)+\frac{\varrho_{x}b_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}
and
\bar{c}_{17}=g_{2}\left(1+\varrho_{x}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)
.

For the final term, we have

\displaystyle T_{9,3}=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(w_{k}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}\right)v_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[w_{k}v_{k}^{\top}\right]+\beta_{k}^{2}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\mathbb{E}[v_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(\Gamma_{21}+\check{R}^{(2,1)}_{k})+\beta_{k}^{2}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\mathbb{E}[v_{k}v_{k}^{\top}],

(by Lemma
D.12
)

where
\|\check{R}^{(2,1)}_{k}\|\leq\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
. We simply bound the second term using Lemma
D.9
, to get
\|\beta_{k}^{2}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\mathbb{E}[v_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]\|\leq 3d\beta_{k}^{2}\varrho_{x}\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{9,3}=\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\Gamma_{21}+R_{k}^{(7)},

where
\|R_{k}^{(7)}\|\leq\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}^{1.5}+3d\beta_{k}^{2}\varrho_{x}\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar_{k}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
. In total, for
T_{9}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{9}=\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\Gamma^{xy}+\beta_{k}(d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv})^{\top}+\alpha_{k}\left(d_{k}^{yw}-d_{k+1}^{yw}\right)+\beta_{k}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})(d_{k}^{yv}-d_{k+1}^{yv})+R_{k}^{(8)},

where
\|R_{k}^{(8)}\|\leq\bar{c}_{18}d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\beta_{k}^{2})+\bar{c}_{19}d\hbar_{k}\alpha_{k}^{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}
. Here

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{18}

\displaystyle=\left(g_{3}+\check{c}_{1}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}+\bar{c}_{16}+3\varrho_{x}\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right),

3.3

\displaystyle=\frac{\beta}{(k+K_{0})^{2}(k+K_{0}+1)}
