Using the above relation, we get

\displaystyle\|R_{k}^{(14)}\|\leq\bar{c}_{41}d^{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{3}}{\alpha_{k}}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}^{1.5}\right)+\bar{c}_{42}d\hbar_{k}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}\zeta_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right).

where

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{41}

\displaystyle=\bar{c}_{39}+\frac{4\beta^{2}\sqrt{3\underline{c}_{7}}}{\alpha(1-\rho)}\breve{c}_{f}+\frac{2\alpha\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}}{\beta^{2}}

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{42}

\displaystyle=\bar{c}_{40}+\frac{4\beta^{2}\sqrt{3\underline{c}_{8}}}{\alpha(1-\rho)}\breve{c}_{f}.

Define
\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}
such that
\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\zeta_{k+1}^{y}=\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y}-\beta_{k}\Delta(\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y})-\beta_{k}(\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y})\Delta^{\top}-\beta_{k}(\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy})A_{12}^{\top}-\beta_{k}A_{12}(\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy})^{\top}+R_{k}^{(14)}
. We have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|\Delta\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\Delta^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|(\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy})A_{12}^{\top}+A_{12}(\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy})^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\frac{1}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|R_{k}^{(14)}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\frac{\|\Delta\|^{2}\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}+\frac{2A_{max}\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\bar{c}_{41}d^{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{3}}{\alpha_{k}}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}^{1.5}\right)+\bar{c}_{42}d\hbar_{k}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}\zeta_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2A_{max}\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}+\bar{c}_{42}d\hbar_{k}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\alpha_{k}}+\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)+\|\Delta\|^{2}\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\bar{c}_{41}d^{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{3}}{\alpha_{k}}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}^{1.5}\right)}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}_{T_{21}}+\frac{\bar{c}_{43}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}+\frac{\bar{c}_{41}d^{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{3}}{\alpha_{k}}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}^{1.5}\right)}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}.

where for the last inequality we used
\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\alpha_{k}}+\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\leq\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\alpha\right)\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}
,
\beta_{k}^{2}\zeta_{k}^{y}\leq\beta\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}
, and

\displaystyle\bar{c}_{43}=2A_{max}\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\bar{c}_{42}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\alpha\right)+\|\Delta\|^{2}\beta\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}.

Next we aim at analyzing
T_{21}
. First, note that
T_{21}\leq\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta},\beta}^{2}\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\leq\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta},\beta}^{2}\hbar_{k}
. Recall that
Q_{\Delta,\beta}
is the solution to the following Lyapunov equation:

\displaystyle\left(\Delta-\frac{\beta^{-1}}{2}I\right)^{\top}Q_{\Delta,\beta}+Q_{\Delta,\beta}\left(\Delta-\frac{\beta^{-1}}{2}I\right)

\displaystyle=I

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\Delta^{\top}Q_{\Delta,\beta}+Q_{\Delta,\beta}\Delta

\displaystyle=I+\beta^{-1}Q_{\Delta,\beta}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\max_{\|x\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}=1}x^{\top}(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)^{\top}Q_{\Delta,\beta}(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)x

\displaystyle=\max_{\|x\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}=1}\left(x^{\top}Q_{\Delta,\beta}x-\beta_{k}x^{\top}(\Delta^{\top}Q_{\Delta,\beta}+Q_{\Delta,\beta}\Delta)x+\beta_{k}^{2}x^{\top}\Delta^{\top}Q_{\Delta,\beta}\Delta x\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1-\beta_{k}\min_{\|x\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}=1}\|x\|^{2}-\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}+\beta_{k}^{2}\max_{\|x\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}=1}\|\Delta x\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}-\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}.

Let
\bar{k}_{3}
to be such that

\displaystyle-\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}\leq-\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq\bar{k}_{3}

(D.12)

Then, for
k\geq\max\{k_{1},\bar{k}_{1},\bar{k}_{2},\bar{k}_{3}\}
we have

\displaystyle\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}-\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}.

In the inequality above, by choosing a larger
\bar{k}_{3}
, instead of
-\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}
, we could get a tighter bound such as
-\frac{5\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{6}
. This is the reason why
c_{0}(\varrho)
in Theorem
4.1
might be arbitrarily large as
\varrho
goes to zero. Hence, we have

\displaystyle T_{21}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta^{y}_{k}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\left(1-\left(\frac{3\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\beta^{-1}\right)\beta_{k}\right)\hbar_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\left(\frac{3\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\beta^{-1}\right)\beta_{k}\right)\hbar_{k}+\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}-\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\left(1-\left(\frac{3\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\beta^{-1}\right)\beta_{k}\right)\hbar_{k}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}-\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}-\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\frac{\zeta_{k}^{y}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}{k+K_{0}}\left(1+\frac{1}{k+K_{0}}\right)^{1+q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}

(Lemma
D.18
)

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}\left(1+q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)\right)\left(1+\frac{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\beta}{4}\right),

where in the last inequality we assumed
\bar{k}_{4}
is such that

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k+K_{0}}\right)^{1+q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}\leq\left(1+\frac{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\beta}{4}\right)\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq\bar{k}_{4}

(D.13)

Hence, for
k\geq\max\{k_{1},\bar{k}_{1},\bar{k}_{2},\bar{k}_{3},\bar{k}_{4}\}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{21}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\left(\frac{3\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\beta^{-1}\right)\beta_{k}\right)\left(1+\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}\left(1+q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)\right)\left(1+\frac{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\beta}{4}\right)\right)\hbar_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\left(\frac{3\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\beta^{-1}\right)\beta_{k}+\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}\left(1+q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)\right)\left(1+\frac{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\beta}{4}\right)\right)\hbar_{k}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\beta_{k}\beta^{-1}q_{\Delta,\beta}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)\left(1+\frac{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\beta}{4}\right)\right)\hbar_{k}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\frac{\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\min(\xi-0.5,1-\xi)}{4}\right)\hbar_{k}

(
q_{\Delta,\beta}=\beta\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}/\left(4+\beta\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}\right)
)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{4}+\frac{\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{16}\right)\hbar_{k}

(
\max_{0.5<\xi<1}\min\{0.5-\xi,1-\xi\}=1/4
)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{11\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{16}\right)\hbar_{k}.

Combining the bounds, we get

\displaystyle T_{21}+

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{c}_{43}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\leq\left(1-\frac{11\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{16}\right)\hbar_{k}+\frac{\bar{c}_{43}\hbar_{k}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}.

Finally, we choose
\bar{k}_{5}
large enough such that

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{c}_{43}\beta_{k}\zeta_{k}^{xy}}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\leq\frac{3\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{16}\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq\bar{k}_{5}

(D.14)

This can always be done since
\zeta_{k}^{xy}=o(\zeta_{k}^{y})
. Thus, for all
k\geq\max\{k_{1},\bar{k}_{1},\bar{k}_{2},\bar{k}_{3},\bar{k}_{4},\bar{k}_{5}\}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\leq\left(1-\frac{\beta_{k}\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{2}\right)\hbar_{k}+\frac{\bar{c}_{41}d^{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{3}}{\alpha_{k}}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}^{1.5}\right)}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}.

Note that
\frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{k}^{3}}{\alpha_{k}}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}^{1.5}\right)}{\zeta_{k+1}^{y}}\leq 4\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha}+\alpha\sqrt{\beta}\right)
. Denote
\bar{c}^{(y)}=4\bar{c}_{41}d^{2}\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha}+\alpha\sqrt{\beta}\right)
. This implies

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(1-\beta_{k}\frac{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}{2}\Bigg)\hbar_{k}+\bar{c}^{(y)}\beta_{k}.

Hence, we have
\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\leq\max\left\{\hbar_{k},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(y)}d^{2}}{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}\right\}
.

Combining the above bounds, we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\}\leq\max\left\{\hbar_{k},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(x)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{4\bar{c}^{(z)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(y)}d^{2}}{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}\right\}.

(D.15)

Define
k_{0}=\max\{k_{1},\bar{k}_{1},\bar{k}_{2},\bar{k}_{3},\bar{k}_{4},\bar{k}_{5}\}
, which is a finite problem dependent number, and

\displaystyle\bar{c}d^{2}=\max\left\{\max_{0\leq k\leq k_{0}}\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(x)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{4\bar{c}^{(z)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(y)}d^{2}}{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}\right\}.

Note that here
\bar{c}
is a bounded, problem dependent constant. To find an absolute bound on
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\}
for
0\leq k\leq k_{0}
, we use Lemma
D.7
as follows. Note that we have
\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y}=\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}-\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{y}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\|\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}\|+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{y}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

(Norm equivalence)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\left(\|\tilde{Y}_{k}\|+2\beta_{k}\|d_{k}^{yv}\|\right)+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{y}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\left(\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]+2\beta_{k}\left(\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}\right)\right)+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{y}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}

(Lemma
D.8
)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\left(\breve{c}d+\frac{4d\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\right)+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{y}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}.

(Lemma
D.7
)

Note that
\beta_{k}/\zeta_{k}^{y}
is an increasing function. Thus, using the above bound, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\leq\frac{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}d}{\zeta_{k_{0}}^{y}}\left(\breve{c}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\right)+\frac{\beta_{k_{0}}}{\zeta_{k_{0}}^{y}}\|\Sigma^{y}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\penalty\ \penalty\ 0\leq k\leq k_{0}

Using similar steps for
\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{\kappa_{Q_{22}}d}{\zeta_{k_{0}}^{x}}\left(\breve{c}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}_{f}\right)+\frac{\alpha_{k_{0}}}{\zeta_{k_{0}}^{x}}\|\Sigma^{x}\|_{Q_{22}}\penalty\ \penalty\ 0\leq k\leq k_{0}

Finally, for the cross term
\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\zeta_{k}^{xy}

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}\|_{Q_{22}}+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{xy}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{22}}\|\tilde{Z}_{k}^{\prime}\|+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{xy}\|_{Q_{22}}

(Norm Equivalence)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{22}}\left(\|\tilde{Z}_{k}\|+\alpha_{k}\|d_{k}^{y}\|+\beta_{k}\|d_{k}^{xv}\|\right)+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{xy}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{22}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]\right)+\alpha_{k}\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ +\beta_{k}\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}\Bigg)+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{xy}\|_{Q_{22}}

(Young’s inequality and Lemma
D.8
)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q_{22}}\Bigg(\frac{\breve{c}d}{2}+\alpha_{k}\frac{2d\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)+\beta_{k}\frac{2d\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\Bigg)+\beta_{k}\|\Sigma^{xy}\|_{Q_{22}}

(Lemma
D.7
)

Again note that
\beta_{k}/\zeta_{k}^{xy}
and
\alpha_{k}/\zeta_{k}^{xy}
are increasing functions of
k
. Thus, we finally get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{\kappa_{Q_{22}}d}{\zeta_{k_{0}}^{xy}}\Bigg(\frac{\breve{c}}{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(\alpha_{k_{0}}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)+\beta_{k_{0}}\right)\Bigg)+\frac{\beta_{k_{0}}}{\zeta_{k_{0}}^{xy}}\|\Sigma^{xy}\|_{Q_{22}}\penalty\ \penalty\ 0\leq k\leq k_{0}

Then by the definition,
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k_{0}}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k_{0}}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k_{0}}\|_{Q_{22}}\}\leq\bar{c}d^{2}
.
Now suppose at time
k\geq k_{0}
, we have

\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,,\beta}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\}=\hbar_{k}\leq\bar{c}d^{2}
.
Then, by (
D.15
), we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\hbar_{k},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(x)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{4\bar{c}^{(z)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(y)}d^{2}}{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\bar{c}d^{2},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(x)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{4\bar{c}^{(z)}d^{2}}{a_{22}},\frac{2\bar{c}^{(y)}d^{2}}{\|Q_{\Delta,\beta}\|^{-1}}\right\}\leq\bar{c}d^{2}.

Hence, by induction,
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},,\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\}\leq\bar{c}d^{2}
for all
k\geq 0
.

∎

Auxiliary lemmas

Since we employ induction to prove our main lemmas, there are two categories of auxiliary lemmas that enable us to achieve this. The first category consists of lemmas that are true irrespective of the hypothesis considered true in the induction, while the second category consists of lemmas that are a consequence of the hypothesis in the induction. For better exposition, we divide this section into these two categories.

Induction independent lemmas

Lemma D.3
.

Consider the recursion of the matrix
L_{k}
in (
C.3
) and (
C.4
). Then
\forall k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\|L_{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle\kappa_{Q_{22}}

Furthermore, define
c_{L}=\max\{\frac{2c_{D}}{a_{22}},\left(\|L_{k_{1}-1}\|_{Q_{22}}+c_{D}\beta_{k_{1}-1}\right)\frac{\alpha_{k_{1}}}{\beta_{k_{1}}}\}
. Then
\forall k\geq k_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|L_{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle c^{L}_{1}\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}},

\displaystyle\|L_{k+1}-L_{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle c^{L}_{2}\alpha_{k}.

where
c^{L}_{1}=c_{L}\kappa_{Q_{22}}
and
c^{L}_{2}=2\max\{\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}},c_{D}\}\kappa_{Q_{22}}
.

Lemma D.4
.

Consider
f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})
and
\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})
as the solution of (
B.4
) for
i=1,2
. We have the following

1.

\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}(\left\|y_{k}\right\|+\left\|x_{k}\right\|)\right)\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{1}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|\right)\right]

2.

\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|\leq\check{h}_{2}(\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|+\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|)

3.

(i)

\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\sqrt{d}+\frac{\check{h}_{3}}{2}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\right)\right)

(ii)

\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\sqrt{d}+\frac{\check{h}_{4}}{2}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\right)\right)

4.

\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|\leq b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|)
.

5.

(i)

\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|))\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

(ii)

\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|))\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{4}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

6.

\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\left(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\right)\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\check{h}_{6}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{5}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

where
\check{h}_{1}=A_{max}(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|)
,

\check{h}_{2}=\frac{2}{1-\rho}A_{max}
,
\check{h}_{3}=2\check{h}_{1}\max\left\{\kappa_{Q_{22}},\sqrt{\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta})}}\right\}
,
\check{h}_{4}=2\check{h}_{1}\max\left\{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta}},\sqrt{\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}}\right\}
,
\check{h}_{5}=\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}\left(\check{h}_{3}+\max\left\{\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta})}}\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}},\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}}\right\}\right)
and
\check{h}_{6}=\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}
.

Lemma D.5
.

Consider the update of the variables in (
B.1
). Then, we have

1.

\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}\leq(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{xx})\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+\hat{h}_{2}^{xx}\alpha_{k}(d+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})
.

2.

\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\leq(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{yy})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\hat{h}_{2}^{yy}\beta_{k}(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2})
.

3.

U_{k+1}\leq(1+\alpha_{k}(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2}))U_{k}+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{2}d
.

4.

\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|\leq\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{3}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})
.

5.

|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|\leq\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{4}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})
.

for some problem dependent constants. The exact expression for the constants are given in the proof of this lemma.

Lemma D.6
.

For all
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle U_{k}\leq

\displaystyle U_{0}\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left[(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\alpha\hat{h}_{2}d\left(\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{1}{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}\right)\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left((k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)\right)

for some problem dependent constants. The exact expression for the constants are given in the proof of this lemma.

Lemma D.7
.

Suppose that Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
are satisfied. Then, there exists a constant
\breve{c}
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\breve{c}d

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\breve{c}d.

Lemma D.8
.

Suppose that Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
are satisfied. Denote
\breve{c}_{f}=\sqrt{b^{2}_{max}+A^{2}_{max}\breve{c}}
. Then, the following relation holds

1.

\left\|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{i}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}\bigg]\right\|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}
.

2.

\left\|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{i}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\bigg]\right\|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}
.

3.

\|d_{k}^{x}\|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}
.

4.

\|d_{k}^{y}\|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}
.

Lemma D.9
.

Suppose that Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
are satisfied. Then, the following relation holds

1.

\mathbb{E}[\|v_{k}\|^{2}]\leq 3d\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
.

2.

\mathbb{E}[\|w_{k}\|^{2}]\leq 3d\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
.

3.

\mathbb{E}[\|u_{k}\|^{2}]\leq 6d\left(1+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
.

Proof of the induction independent lemmas

Proof of Lemma
D.3
.

From Lemma
D.17
, we have that for
k\geq k_{L}
,

\displaystyle L_{k+1}

\displaystyle=((I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})L_{k}+\beta_{k}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}B^{k}_{11})(I-\beta_{k}B^{k}_{11})^{-1}

\displaystyle=(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})L_{k}+\beta_{k}D(L_{k})

where
D(L_{k})=(A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}+(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})L_{k})B^{k}_{11}(I-\beta_{k}B^{k}_{11})^{-1}
. Note that because of the choice of
k_{L}
, we have
\|L_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq 1\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall k\geq 0
, which implies
\|D(L_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}\leq c_{D}
. We will prove the lemma by induction.
\|L_{k_{1}}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k_{1}}}{\alpha_{k_{1}}}
by construction. Assume that
\|L_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}
for some
k\geq k_{1}
. Then for
k+1
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}-\|L_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}-(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\|L_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}-c_{D}\beta_{k}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}-(1-\alpha_{k}a_{22})\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}-c_{D}\beta_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}-\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}+c_{L}a_{22}\beta_{k}-c_{D}\beta_{k}

\displaystyle=c_{L}\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\beta_{k+1}}{\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{k}}+a_{22}-\frac{c_{D}}{c_{L}}\right)

\displaystyle=c_{L}\beta_{k}\left(a_{22}-\frac{c_{D}}{c_{L}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{k}}(1-\frac{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}\beta_{k}})\right)

\displaystyle\geq c_{L}\beta_{k}\left(\frac{a_{22}}{2}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{k}}(1-\frac{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}\beta_{k}})\right)

Substituting the values for
\beta_{k}
and
\alpha_{k}
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}\beta_{k}}=\big(\frac{k+K_{0}}{k+K_{0}+1}\big)^{1-\xi}=\left(1+\frac{1}{k+K_{0}}\right)^{\xi-1}\geq\exp{\frac{\xi-1}{k+K_{0}}}\geq 1-\frac{1-\xi}{k+K_{0}}

Using this, we get:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha_{k}}(1-\frac{\alpha_{k}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}\beta_{k}})=\frac{(k+K_{0})^{\xi}}{\alpha}\big(1-\big(\frac{k+K_{0}}{k+K_{0}+1}\big)^{1-\xi}\big)\leq\frac{1-\xi}{\alpha(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}}

Note that
k_{1}
is large enough that
\frac{1-\xi}{\alpha(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}}\leq\frac{a_{22}}{2}
. Thus, we get,

\displaystyle\|L_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{c_{L}\beta_{k+1}}{\alpha_{k+1}}

By norm equivalence we get,

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\|L_{k}\|\leq\frac{c^{L}_{1}\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}

where
c^{L}_{1}=c_{L}\kappa_{Q_{22}}
.
For the second part we have,

\displaystyle\|L_{k+1}-L_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle=\|-\alpha_{k}A_{22}L_{k}+\beta_{k}D_{k}(L_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\alpha_{k}\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}+c_{D}\beta_{k}\leq 2\max\{\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}},c_{D}\}\alpha_{k}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\|L_{k+1}-L_{k}\|\leq c^{L}_{2}\alpha_{k}

where
c^{L}_{2}=2\max\{\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}},c_{D}\}\kappa_{Q_{22}}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
D.4
.

1.

By Lemma
D.14
, for
\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(O_{l})|O_{o}=o]}-\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i1}(O_{l})-A_{i1}|O_{0}=o]}\right)y_{k}-\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i2}(O_{l})-A_{i2}|O_{0}=o]}\right)x_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(O_{l})|O_{o}=o]}\right\|+\left\|\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1}|O_{0}=o]}\right)y_{k}\right\|+\left\|\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i2}(O_{l})-A_{i2}|O_{0}=o]}\right)x_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(O_{l})|O_{o}=o]}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1}|O_{0}=o]}\right\|\left\|y_{k}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i2}(O_{l})-A_{i2}|O_{0}=o]}\right\|\left\|x_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[\max_{o\in\mathcal{S}}\|b_{i}(o)\|+A_{max}\left\|y_{k}\right\|+A_{max}\left\|x_{k}\right\|\right]

(Lemma
D.15
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}\left\|y_{k}\right\|+A_{max}\left\|x_{k}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}[\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|+(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|)\|\hat{y}_{k}\|]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{1}[\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|]\right].

2.

\displaystyle\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|

\displaystyle=\left\|-\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1}|O_{0}=o]}\right)(y_{k+1}-y_{k})-\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2}|O_{0}=o]}\right)(x_{k+1}-x_{k})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1}|O_{0}=o]}\right)(y_{k+1}-y_{k})\right\|+\left\|\left(\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2}|O_{0}=o]}\right)(x_{k+1}-x_{k})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1}|O_{0}=o]}\right\|\left\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2}|O_{0}=o]}\right\|\left\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}A_{max}\left\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\right\|+\frac{2}{1-\rho}A_{max}\left\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle=\check{h}_{2}(\left\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\right\|+\left\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\right\|).

3.

(i)

\displaystyle\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\langle\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k}),Q_{22}\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{1}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\frac{\check{h}_{1}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta})}}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\frac{\check{h}_{1}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}\left(\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)\right)

(ii)

Similar to the previous part, we get

\displaystyle\|\hat{f}_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{4}\left(\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)\right)

4.

\displaystyle\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|=

\displaystyle\|b_{i}(o)-(A_{i1}(o)-A_{i1})y-(A_{i2}(o)-A_{i2})x\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|b_{i}(o)\|+\|A_{i1}(o)-A_{i1}\|\|y_{k}\|+\|A_{i2}(o)-A_{i2}\|\|x_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\|b_{i}(o^{\prime})\|+2A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+2A_{max}\|x_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+2A_{max}\|x_{k}\|

5.

(i)

\displaystyle\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\langle f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k}),Q_{22}f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+2A_{max}\|x_{k}\|\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|+2A_{max}\|\hat{x}_{k}-A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}y_{k}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+2A_{max}(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|)\|\hat{y}_{k}\|+2A_{max}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\frac{2A_{max}(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|)}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta})}}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\frac{2A_{max}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}\left(\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)

(ii)

Similar to the previous part, we get

\displaystyle\|f_{i}(o,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{4}\left(\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\right)

6.

\displaystyle\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}

\displaystyle+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}(\|\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}})

\displaystyle\leq\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}\left(\|\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}\Bigg(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{\Delta})}}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\check{h}_{6}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{5}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.5
.

1.

For
\hat{x}_{k+1}
, from the proof of Lemma
D.7
we have the following recursion

\displaystyle\hat{x}_{k+1}=

\displaystyle(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})).

Hence,

\displaystyle\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\|I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}}_{T_{1}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\|f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}}_{T_{2}}

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}\underbrace{\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}}_{T_{3}}

(D.16)

For
T_{1}
we have

\displaystyle\|I-\alpha_{k}A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq 1+\alpha_{k}\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}.

For
T_{2}
, using Lemma
D.4
we have
T_{2}\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})
.

For
T_{3}
, using Lemma
D.4
we have
T_{3}\leq\check{h}_{6}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{5}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})
.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\alpha_{k}\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}})\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right)

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\left(\check{h}_{6}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{5}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{2}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

(D.17)

where
\hat{h}^{x}_{1}=\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}+\check{h}_{3}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\check{h}_{5}
and
\hat{h}^{x}_{2}=\max\left\{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}b_{max}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\check{h}_{6},\check{h}_{3}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\check{h}_{5}\right\}
. Now squaring both sides of (
D.17
), we get:

\displaystyle\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)^{2}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}^{2}(\hat{h}^{x}_{2})^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})^{2}+2\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{2}\left(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)^{2}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}^{2}(\hat{h}^{x}_{2})^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})^{2}+\hat{h}^{x}_{2}\left(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)(\alpha_{k}d+2\alpha_{k}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\alpha_{k}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})

(By Cauchy-Schwartz)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\alpha_{k}\left(\alpha(\hat{h}^{x}_{1})^{2}+2\hat{h}^{x}_{1}+2\hat{h}^{x}_{2}\left(1+\alpha\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)\right)\right)\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+2\alpha_{k}\alpha(\hat{h}^{x}_{2})^{2}(d+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}^{x}_{2}\left(1+\alpha\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)(d+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{xx}\right)\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{2}^{xx}(d+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})

(D.18)

where
\hat{h}_{1}^{xx}=\alpha(\hat{h}^{x}_{1})^{2}+2\hat{h}^{x}_{1}+2\hat{h}^{x}_{2}\left(1+\alpha\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)
and
\hat{h}_{2}^{xx}=2\alpha(\hat{h}^{x}_{2})^{2}+\hat{h}^{x}_{2}\left(1+\alpha\hat{h}^{x}_{1}\right)
.

2.

For
\hat{y}_{k+1}
, we have the following recursion

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{k+1}

\displaystyle=(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k}+\beta_{k}f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\beta_{k}A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}.

Taking norm on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\leq\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\beta_{k}\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\beta_{k}\|A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}.

(D.19)

We have

\displaystyle\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq(1+\beta_{k}\|\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}.

Furthermore, using Lemma
D.4
, we have
\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{4}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})
.

Finally, we have:

\displaystyle\|A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\beta_{k}\|\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\beta_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{4}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right)

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{y})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}).

(D.20)

where
\hat{h}^{y}_{1}=\|\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}}+\check{h}_{4}
and
\hat{h}^{y}_{2}=\max\left\{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}b_{max},\check{h}_{4}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\frac{\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}}{\sqrt{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}}\right\}
. Squaring both sides of (
D.20
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{y})^{2}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}(\hat{h}_{2}^{y})^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}})^{2}+2\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{y})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}})

\displaystyle\leq(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{y})^{2}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}(\hat{h}_{2}^{y})^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}})^{2}+\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(1+\beta\hat{h}_{1}^{y})(\beta_{k}d+2\beta_{k}\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\beta_{k}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2})

(by Cauchy-Schwartz)

\displaystyle\leq(1+\beta_{k}(2\hat{h}_{1}^{y}+\beta(\hat{h}_{1}^{y})^{2}+2\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(1+\beta\hat{h}_{1}^{y})))\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+2\beta_{k}\beta(\hat{h}_{2}^{y})^{2}(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(1+\beta\hat{h}_{1}^{y})(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2})

\displaystyle=(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{yy})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\hat{h}_{2}^{yy}\beta_{k}(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}).

(D.21)

where
\hat{h}_{1}^{yy}=2\hat{h}_{2}^{y}+\beta(\hat{h}_{1}^{y})^{2}+2\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(1+\beta\hat{h}_{1}^{y})
and
\hat{h}_{2}^{yy}=2\beta(\hat{h}_{2}^{y})^{2}+\hat{h}_{2}^{y}(1+\beta\hat{h}_{1}^{y})
.

3.

Summing (
D.18
) and (
D.21
), we get

\displaystyle U_{k+1}\leq(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{1})U_{k}+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{2}(d+U_{k})

\displaystyle=(1+\alpha_{k}(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2}))U_{k}+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{2}d

where
\hat{h}_{1}=\max\left\{\hat{h}_{1}^{xx},\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\hat{h}_{1}^{yy}\right\}
and
\hat{h}_{2}=\max\left\{\hat{h}_{2}^{xx},\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\hat{h}_{2}^{yy}\right\}
.

4.

\displaystyle\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|

\displaystyle=\alpha_{k}\|A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}-f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}\left(\|A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}\|+\|f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}\left(\|A_{22}\|.\|\hat{x}_{k}\|+b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{3}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

where
\hat{h}_{3}=\max\left\{\|A_{22}\|+\check{h}_{3},b_{max}\right\}
.

5.

\displaystyle\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|

\displaystyle=\beta_{k}\|\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}-f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}(\|\Delta\|\|\hat{y}_{k}\|+\|A_{12}\|\|\hat{x}_{k}\|+\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|)

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}\left(\|\Delta\|\|\hat{y}_{k}\|+\|A_{12}\|\|\hat{x}_{k}\|+b_{max}\sqrt{d}+\check{h}_{4}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right)

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{4}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})

where
\hat{h}_{4}=\max\left\{\|\Delta\|+\check{h}_{4},\|A_{12}\|+\check{h}_{4},b_{max}\right\}
.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.6
.

From Lemma
D.5
, for all
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle U_{k}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha_{k-1}(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2}))U_{k-1}+\alpha_{k-1}\hat{h}_{2}d

\displaystyle\leq\Pi_{i=0}^{k-1}(1+\alpha_{i}(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2}))U_{0}+\hat{h}_{2}d\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{i}\Pi_{j=i+1}^{k-1}(1+\alpha_{j}(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2}))

\displaystyle\leq U_{0}\exp\left((\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{i}\right)+\hat{h}_{2}d\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{i}\exp\left((\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\sum_{j=i+1}^{k-1}\alpha_{j}\right).

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\exp\left((\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{i}\right)=

\displaystyle\exp\left((\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\frac{\alpha}{(i+K_{0})^{\xi}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left((\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\left(\frac{\alpha}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\int_{x=0}^{k}\frac{\alpha}{(x+K_{0})^{\xi}}dx\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\left[\frac{\alpha}{(1-\xi)(x+K_{0})^{\xi-1}}\right]_{x=0}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left[(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right]\right).

Similarly, for the second term,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{i}\exp\left((\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\sum_{j=i+1}^{k-1}\alpha_{j}\right)\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\frac{\alpha}{(i+K_{0})^{\xi}}\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left[(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-(i+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{(i+K_{0})^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(i+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{(i+K_{0})^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(i+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg[\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{(i+K_{0})^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(i+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg[\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\int_{x=0}^{k}\frac{1}{(x+K_{0})^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(x+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg[\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\frac{1}{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(x+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg]_{x=0}^{k}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg[\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\left(\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{1}{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}\right)\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left((k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)\right).

Putting things together, we have

\displaystyle U_{k}\leq

\displaystyle U_{0}\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left[(k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\alpha\hat{h}_{2}d\left(\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{1}{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}\right)\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left((k+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)\right)

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.7
.

Recall that
Q_{22}
and
Q_{\Delta}
were defined such that

\displaystyle A_{22}^{\top}Q_{22}+Q_{22}A_{22}=I

\displaystyle\Delta^{\top}Q_{\Delta}+Q_{\Delta}\Delta=I.

Note that by Assumption
3.1
, we can always find positive-definite matrices
Q_{22}
and
Q_{\Delta}
which satisfy the above equations. Furthermore, for all
k>k_{C}
, by Lemma
D.21
we have
\|(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}\leq(1-a_{22}\alpha_{k})
and
\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\leq(1-\delta\beta_{k})
for positive constants
a_{22}=\frac{1}{2\|Q_{22}\|}
and
\delta=\frac{1}{2\|Q_{\Delta}\|}
. Throughout the proof, we consider
k>k_{C}
.

Recall
V_{k}=\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}]
and
W_{k}=\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}]
.

First, we handle the
V_{k}
term.

\displaystyle x_{k+1}=

\displaystyle x_{k}-\alpha_{k}(A_{21}y_{k}+A_{22}x_{k})+\alpha_{k}f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})

\displaystyle x_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}y_{k+1}=

\displaystyle x_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}y_{k}-\alpha_{k}A_{22}(x_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}y_{k})+\alpha_{k}f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(y_{k+1}-y_{k})

\displaystyle\hat{x}_{k+1}=

\displaystyle(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})+\beta_{k}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))

\displaystyle\hat{x}_{k+1}=

\displaystyle(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\underbrace{(A_{11}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})}_{\Delta}\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))

Taking norm square and expectation thereafter, we get:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]+\underbrace{\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k},A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}),A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k},f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{5}}

•

For
T_{1}
, by Lemma
D.4
we have

\displaystyle\|f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}\leq 3(\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b^{2}_{max}d+\check{h}_{3}^{2}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}})),

to get:

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{1}(d+V_{k}+W_{k}).

where
\breve{c}_{1}=3\max\{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b^{2}_{max},\check{h}_{3}^{2}\}
.

•

For
T_{2}
, again we use Lemma
D.4

to get:

\displaystyle T_{2}\leq

\displaystyle 3\beta_{k}^{2}\left(\check{h}_{6}^{2}d+\check{h}_{5}^{2}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\breve{c}_{2}\beta_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

where
\breve{c}_{2}=3\max\{\check{h}_{6}^{2},\check{h}_{5}^{2}\}
.

•

For
T_{3}
, we apply Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to get:

\displaystyle T_{3}\leq 2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\left((\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})-f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\right)\|_{Q_{22}}]

Using AM-GM inequality
2ab\leq\frac{a^{2}}{\eta}+b^{2}\eta
with
\eta=\frac{2\beta_{k}}{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}
, we get:

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}]+\frac{4\beta_{k}^{2}}{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}\mathbb{E}[\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\left((\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})-f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\right)\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2}V_{k}+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

(D.22)

where
\breve{c}_{3}=\frac{4\breve{c}_{2}}{a_{22}}
.

•

For
T_{4}
, again applying Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we get:

\displaystyle T_{4}\leq 2\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\|f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}]

Using AM-GM inequality and after some simple calculation, we get:

\displaystyle T_{4}\leq\breve{c}_{4}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

where
\breve{c}_{4}=\breve{c}_{1}+\breve{c}_{2}
.

•

For
T_{5}
, we break it down into two terms:

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle=2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k},f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{x}_{k},f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{51}}\underbrace{-2\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\langle A_{22}\hat{x}_{k},f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{52}}

By Remark
Remark
, we have a unique function
\hat{f}_{2}(O,x_{k},y_{k})
such that,

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{2}(O,x_{k},y_{k})=f_{2}(O,x_{k},y_{k})+\sum_{o^{\prime}\in S}P(o^{\prime}|O)\hat{f}_{2}(o^{\prime},x_{k},y_{k}),

where
P(O^{\prime}|O)
is the transition probability corresponding to the Markov chain
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{51}=

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\hat{x}_{k},\hat{f}_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\sum_{o^{\prime}\in S}P(o^{\prime}|O_{k})\hat{f}_{2}(o^{\prime},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\hat{x}_{k},\hat{f}_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\hat{x}_{k},\hat{f}_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})+\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\hat{x}_{k},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{x}_{k},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{\bar{d}_{k}^{x}}-2\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{x}_{k+1},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{x}_{k+1},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{511}}+\underbrace{2\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(\hat{x}_{k+1}^{\top}-\hat{x}_{k}^{\top}),\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{22}}]}_{T_{512}}

For
T_{511}
, we use Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the fact that
\hat{f}_{2}
is Lipschitz, to get:

\displaystyle T_{511}\leq

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{2}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}(\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|+\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|)]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\breve{c}_{5}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}(\hat{h}_{3}\alpha_{k}+\hat{h}_{4}\beta_{k})(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})]

(Lemma
D.5
)

where
\breve{c}_{5}=2\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\check{h}_{2}
. Applying AM-GM to the previous inequality, we get

\displaystyle T_{511}\leq

\displaystyle 0.5\alpha_{k}^{2}\left(\hat{h}_{3}+\hat{h}_{4}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\breve{c}_{5}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 0.5\alpha_{k}^{2}\left(\hat{h}_{3}+\hat{h}_{4}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\breve{c}_{5}\mathbb{E}\left[(1+\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{xx})V_{k}+\hat{h}_{2}^{xx}\alpha_{k}(d+W_{k})+3(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})\right]

(Lemma
D.5
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{6}(d+V_{k}+W_{k}),

where
\breve{c}_{6}=0.5\left(\hat{h}_{3}+\hat{h}_{4}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\breve{c}_{5}\max\{4+\alpha\hat{h}_{1}^{xx},3+\hat{h}_{2}^{xx}\}
.

Similarly, for
T_{512}
, we use the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to get:

\displaystyle T_{512}\leq

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|-A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}+f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}]

Applying AM-GM inequality
2ab\leq\frac{a^{2}}{\eta}+b^{2}\eta
with
\eta=\frac{1-\rho}{2}
, we get:

\displaystyle T_{512}\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{2}{1-\rho}\|-A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}+f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1-\rho}{2}\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{7}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

where
\breve{c}_{7}=\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(\max\{4\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+12\check{h}_{3}^{2},12\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b_{max}^{2}\}+\frac{2\beta^{2}\breve{c}_{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)+\frac{6\gamma_{\max}(Q_{22})}{(1-\rho)}\max\left\{b_{max}^{2}\gamma_{max},\frac{\check{h}_{3}^{2}}{4}\right\}
. Here, we used

\displaystyle\|-A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}+f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|-A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}+f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta^{2}_{k}}{\alpha^{2}_{k}}\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}(-(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max\{4\|A_{22}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+12\check{h}_{3}^{2},12\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b_{max}^{2}\}+\frac{2\beta^{2}\breve{c}_{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

(Lemma
D.4
)

Furthermore, by Lemma
D.4
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6\gamma_{\max}(Q_{22})}{(1-\rho)^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[b_{max}^{2}\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})d+\frac{\check{h}_{3}^{2}}{4}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6\gamma_{\max}(Q_{22})}{(1-\rho)^{2}}\max\left\{b_{max}^{2}\gamma_{max},\frac{\check{h}_{3}^{2}}{4}\right\}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

Finally, for
T_{52}
, using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and then AM-GM inequality, we have:

\displaystyle T_{52}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\|A_{22}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}])

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\|A_{22}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}]+3\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b_{max}^{2}d+3\check{h}_{3}^{2}(\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}))

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}^{2}\max\left\{\|A_{22}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+3\check{h}_{3}^{2},3\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b_{max}^{2}\right\}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

\displaystyle\leq\breve{c}_{8}\alpha_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k}),

where
\breve{c}_{8}=\max\left\{\|A_{22}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}+3\check{h}_{3}^{2},3\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})b_{max}^{2}\right\}
.

Finally, by Lemma
D.21
we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\alpha_{k}A_{22})\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]\leq(1-a_{22}\alpha_{k})V_{k}.

Combining everything, we have:

\displaystyle V_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-a_{22}\alpha_{k})V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{1}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\breve{c}_{2}\beta_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2}V_{k}+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

\displaystyle+\breve{c}_{4}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{6}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{7}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\breve{c}_{8}\alpha_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

\displaystyle+2\alpha_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{9}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}+2(\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k-1})\bar{d}_{k}^{x},

(D.23)

where
\breve{c}_{9}=\breve{c}_{1}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\breve{c}_{2}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{4}+\breve{c}_{6}+\breve{c}_{7}+\breve{c}_{8}
.

We bound the last term as follows:

\displaystyle|(\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k-1})\bar{d}_{k}^{x}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi}{\alpha}\alpha_{k}^{2}|\bar{d}_{k}^{x}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi}{\alpha}\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{2}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{22}}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi}{\alpha}\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}\left(\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})}\sqrt{d}+\frac{\check{h}_{3}}{2}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\right)\right]\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\xi}{(1-\rho)\alpha}\max\left\{b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})},\frac{\check{h}_{3}}{2}\right\}\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi}{(1-\rho)\alpha}\max\left\{b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})},\frac{\check{h}_{3}}{2}\right\}\alpha_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+3(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})]

\displaystyle=\frac{\breve{c}_{10}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

(D.24)

where
\breve{c}_{10}=\frac{8\xi}{(1-\rho)\alpha}\max\left\{b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})},\frac{\check{h}_{3}}{2}\right\}
. Thus we get:

\displaystyle V_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{9}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}

\displaystyle+\check{c}_{10}\alpha_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{11}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x},

(D.25)

where
\breve{c}_{11}=\breve{c}_{9}+\breve{c}_{10}
.

Next, we handle
W_{k}
. We have

\displaystyle y_{k+1}

\displaystyle=y_{k}-\beta_{k}(A_{11}y_{k}+A_{12}x_{k})+\beta_{k}f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{k+1}

\displaystyle=\hat{y}_{k}-\beta_{k}((A_{11}-A_{12}A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\hat{y}_{k}+A_{12}\hat{x}_{k})+\beta_{k}f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{k+1}

\displaystyle=(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k}+\beta_{k}f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\beta_{k}A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}

Taking norm square and expectation thereafter, we get:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}]+\underbrace{\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}}_{T_{6}}+\underbrace{\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{7}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k},A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{8}}\underbrace{-2\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\langle f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}),A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{9}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k},f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{10}}.

•

For
T_{6}
, using Lemma
D.4
we have

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle\leq 3\beta_{k}^{2}(\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})b_{max}^{2}d+\check{h}_{4}^{2}(V_{k}+W_{k}))

\displaystyle=\breve{c}_{12}\beta_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k}),

where
\breve{c}_{12}=3\max\{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})b_{max}^{2},\check{h}_{4}^{2}\}
.

•

For
T_{7}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\beta_{k}^{2}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}V_{k}

•

For
T_{8}
, using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have:

\displaystyle T_{8}\leq

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|I-\beta_{k}\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]

(Assumption on
k
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{k}\delta}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}]+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}}{\delta}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{k}\delta}{2}W_{k}+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}V_{k}

where for the one to last inequality we used AM-GM inequality
2ab\leq\frac{a^{2}}{\eta}+\eta b^{2}
with
\eta=\frac{\delta}{2}
.

•

For
T_{9}
, we have the following.

\displaystyle T_{9}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}.\|A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\|A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}^{2}[\check{c}_{12}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}V_{k}]

•

For
T_{10}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{10}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{y}_{k},f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{101}}\underbrace{-2\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\langle\Delta\hat{y}_{k},f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{102}}

Similar to analysis of
T_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{101}=

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{y}_{k},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{\bar{d}_{k}^{y}}-2\beta_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{y}_{k+1},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle\hat{y}_{k+1},\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{1011}}+\underbrace{2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle(\hat{y}_{k+1}^{\top}-\hat{y}_{k}^{\top}),\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\rangle_{Q_{\Delta}}]}_{T_{1012}}.

For
T_{1011}
we have

\displaystyle T_{1011}\leq

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\check{h}_{2}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}(\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|+\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|)]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\check{h}_{2}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}(\alpha_{k}\hat{h}_{3}+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{4})\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})]

(Lemma
D.5
)

Applying AM-GM to the previous inequality, we get

\displaystyle T_{1011}\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\left(\hat{h}_{3}+\hat{h}_{4}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\check{h}_{2}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hat{y}_{k+1}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\left(\hat{h}_{3}+\hat{h}_{4}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\check{h}_{2}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[(1+\beta_{k}\hat{h}_{1}^{yy})\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\hat{h}_{2}^{yy}\beta_{k}(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2})

\displaystyle+3(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})\Bigg]

(Lemma
D.5
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{13}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

where
\breve{c}_{13}=\left(\hat{h}_{3}+\hat{h}_{4}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\check{h}_{2}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\max\{4+\alpha\hat{h}_{1}^{yy},3+\hat{h}_{2}^{yy}\}
. For
T_{1012}
we have:

\displaystyle T_{1012}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta_{k}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k+1}^{\top}-\hat{y}_{k}^{\top}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\|\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]

(by Cauchy-Schwartz)

\displaystyle=2\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|-\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}[\|\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]]

Applying AM-GM inequality
2ab\leq\frac{a^{2}}{\eta}+b^{2}\eta
with
\eta=\frac{1-\rho}{2}
, we get:

\displaystyle T_{1012}\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{2}{1-\rho}\|-\Delta\hat{y}_{k}+f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-A_{12}\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\frac{1-\rho}{2}\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\|\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\rho}\beta_{k}^{2}\left(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\breve{c}_{12}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}\right)(d+\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}])

\displaystyle+\frac{(1-\rho)\beta_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{14}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

where
\breve{c}_{14}=\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\breve{c}_{12}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}\right)+\frac{6}{1-\rho}\left(b_{max}^{2}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})+\frac{\check{h}_{4}^{2}}{4}\right)
. Here for bounding

\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\right]
, we use Lemma
D.4
.

For
T_{102}
we have:

\displaystyle T_{102}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta\hat{y}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}+\|f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}]

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{15}(d+W_{k}+V_{k})

where
\breve{c}_{15}=\|\Delta\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+\breve{c}_{12}
.

Now, by definition of
Q_{\Delta}
, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\beta_{k}\Delta)\hat{y}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}]\leq(1-\delta\beta_{k})W_{k}.

Combining everything, we have:

\displaystyle W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\delta\beta_{k})W_{k}+\beta_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{12}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}^{2}\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}V_{k}+\frac{\beta_{k}\delta}{2}W_{k}+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}V_{k}

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}^{2}\check{c}_{12}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}^{2}\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}V_{k}+\beta_{k}\alpha_{k}\breve{c}_{13}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{14}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{15}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+2\beta_{k}(\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{2})W_{k}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{16}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}V_{k}+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}+2(\beta_{k}-\beta_{k-1})\bar{d}_{k}^{y},

(D.26)

where
\breve{c}_{16}=\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\left(\breve{c}_{12}+2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\frac{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})}+\breve{c}_{14}+\breve{c}_{15}\right)+\breve{c}_{13}
.

We bound the last term as follows:

\displaystyle|(\beta_{k}-\beta_{k-1})\bar{d}_{k}^{y}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\beta}\beta_{k}^{2}|\bar{d}_{k}^{y}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\beta}\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|_{Q_{\Delta}}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\beta}\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\left(\frac{2}{1-\rho}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}\sqrt{d}+\frac{\check{h}_{4}}{2}\left(\left\|\hat{x}_{k}\right\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}\right)\right]\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{(1-\rho)\beta}\max\left\{b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})},\frac{\check{h}_{4}}{2}\right\}\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}(\sqrt{d}+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}})]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{(1-\rho)\beta}\max\left\{b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})},\frac{\check{h}_{4}}{2}\right\}\beta_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2}+3(d+\|\hat{x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}}^{2}+\|\hat{y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta}}^{2})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\breve{c}_{17}}{2}\beta_{k}^{2}(d+V_{k}+W_{k}),

(D.27)

where
\breve{c}_{17}=\frac{8}{(1-\rho)\beta}\max\left\{b_{max}\sqrt{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22})},\frac{\check{h}_{4}}{2}\right\}
. Thus we get:

\displaystyle W_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{2})W_{k}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{18}(1+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}V_{k}+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y},

(D.28)

where
\breve{c}_{18}=\breve{c}_{16}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{17}
.

Then, by adding (
D.3.2
) and (
D.28
) we get,

\displaystyle U_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{2})V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{11}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}

\displaystyle+(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{2})W_{k}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{18}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\beta_{k}\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}V_{k}+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}

But we had
k>k_{C}
, and hence
\frac{2\|A_{12}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})}{\gamma_{min}(Q_{22})\delta}\beta_{k}+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}\leq\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{4}
, and
\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}\leq\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4}
. Hence,

\displaystyle U_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{4})V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\breve{c}_{11}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}d+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}

\displaystyle+(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4})W_{k}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{18}(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}.

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{4})V_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{2}(\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18})(d+V_{k}+W_{k})+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}d+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}

\displaystyle+(1-\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4})W_{k}+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}.

It is sufficient to have that
(\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18})\alpha_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{a_{22}\alpha_{k}}{4}
and
(\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18})\alpha_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{\delta\beta_{k}}{4}
. Therefore, it is further sufficient to have
(\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18})\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{{(k+1)}^{2\xi}}\leq\min\{\frac{a_{22}\alpha}{4},\frac{\delta\beta}{4}\}\frac{1}{k+1}
, which happens for

k\geq\left((\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18})\alpha^{2}/\min\{\frac{a_{22}\alpha}{4},\frac{\delta\beta}{4}\}\right)^{\frac{1}{2\xi-1}}
.

We define
\breve{c}_{19}=\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18}
. Then, for all
k\geq\max\left\{k_{C},\left((\breve{c}_{11}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\breve{c}_{18})\alpha^{2}/\min\{\frac{a_{22}\alpha}{4},\frac{\delta\beta}{4}\}\right)^{\frac{1}{2\xi-1}}\right\}:=k_{2}
, we have,

\displaystyle U_{k+1}\leq

\displaystyle V_{k}+W_{k}+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}+\breve{c}_{19}\alpha_{k}^{2}d+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}d

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle U_{k}+2\alpha_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{x}+2\beta_{k-1}\bar{d}_{k}^{y}-2\alpha_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{x}-2\beta_{k}\bar{d}_{k+1}^{y}+\breve{c}_{19}\alpha_{k}^{2}d+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}d.

(D.29)

Summing from
k_{2}
to
K
, we have

\displaystyle U_{K+1}\leq U_{k_{2}}+2\alpha_{k_{2}-1}\bar{d}_{k_{2}}^{x}-2\alpha_{K}\bar{d}_{K+1}^{x}+2\beta_{k_{2}-1}\bar{d}_{k_{2}}^{y}-2\beta_{K}\bar{d}_{K+1}^{y}+\breve{c}_{19}d\sum_{k=k_{2}}^{K}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\breve{c}_{3}d\sum_{k=k_{2}}^{K}\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}.

From (
D.24
), we have
|\bar{d}_{k}^{x}|\leq\frac{\breve{c}_{10}\alpha}{2\xi}(d+U_{k})
and from (
D.27
) we have
\bar{d}_{k}^{y}\leq\frac{\breve{c}_{17}\beta}{2}(d+U_{k})
. By the choice of
k_{C}
, we have
\alpha_{k}\frac{\breve{c}_{10}\alpha}{\xi}\leq 0.3
and
\beta_{k}\breve{c}_{17}\beta\leq 0.3
. Since we assume
k\geq k_{2}-1
, and we have
k_{2}>k_{C}
, we get for all
K\geq k_{2}

\displaystyle U_{K+1}\leq

\displaystyle U_{k_{2}}+2\beta_{k_{2}-1}\bar{d}_{k_{2}}^{y}+2\alpha_{k_{2}-1}\bar{d}_{k_{2}}^{x}+0.6(d+U_{K+1})+\breve{c}_{19}d\sum_{k=k_{2}}^{K}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\breve{c}_{3}d\sum_{k=k_{2}}^{K}\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}}{\alpha_{k}}

\displaystyle\implies 0.4U_{K+1}\leq

\displaystyle U_{k_{2}}+2\beta_{k_{2}-1}\bar{d}_{k_{2}}^{y}+2\alpha_{k_{2}-1}\bar{d}_{k_{2}}^{x}+0.6d+\frac{\breve{c}_{19}\alpha^{2}}{2\xi-1}d+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta^{2}}{\alpha(1-\xi)}d

\displaystyle\implies 0.4U_{K+1}\leq

\displaystyle U_{k_{2}}+0.6(d+U_{k_{2}})+0.6d+\frac{\breve{c}_{19}\alpha^{2}}{2\xi-1}d+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta^{2}}{\alpha(1-\xi)}d

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1.2d+1.6U_{k_{2}}+\frac{\breve{c}_{19}\alpha^{2}}{2\xi-1}d+\frac{\breve{c}_{3}\beta^{2}}{\alpha(1-\xi)}d

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]\leq

\displaystyle 2(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2})\max\{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22}),\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})\}(\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}])

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2})

\displaystyle\max\{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22}),\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})\}\left(3d+4U_{k_{2}}+\frac{2.5\breve{c}_{19}\alpha^{2}}{2\xi-1}d+\frac{2.5\breve{c}_{3}\beta^{2}}{\alpha(1-\xi)}d\right)

\displaystyle=\breve{c}_{20}U_{k_{2}}+\breve{c}_{21}d,

(D.30)

for obvious choice of
\breve{c}_{20}
and
\breve{c}_{21}
. We use Lemma
D.6
to upper bound
U_{k_{2}}
as

\displaystyle U_{k_{2}}\leq

\displaystyle U_{0}\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left[(k_{2}+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\hat{h}_{2}d\alpha\left(\frac{1}{K_{0}^{\xi}}+\frac{1}{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}\right)\exp\left(\frac{(\hat{h}_{1}+\hat{h}_{2})\alpha}{(1-\xi)}\left((k_{2}+K_{0})^{1-\xi}-K_{0}^{1-\xi}\right)\right).

Note that
U_{0}=\mathcal{O}(d)
. Hence,

\displaystyle U_{K}\leq d\left(\breve{c}_{20}U_{k_{2}}/d+\breve{c}_{21}\right).

For the second part of the Lemma, recall that
\tilde{x}_{k}=\hat{x}_{k}+L_{k}y_{k}
and
\tilde{y}_{k}=y_{k}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2(\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}]+\|L_{k}\|^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}])

\displaystyle\leq 2(\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}]+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}])

(Lemma
D.3
)

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\max\{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22}),\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})\}(\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{x}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}]+(1+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2})E[\|\hat{y}_{k}\|^{2}_{Q_{\Delta}}])

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2})\max\{\gamma_{max}(Q_{22}),\gamma_{max}(Q_{\Delta})\}U_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(1+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2})d}{(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2})}\left(\breve{c}_{20}U_{k_{2}}/d+\breve{c}_{21}\right).

Define
\breve{c}=\max\left(1,\frac{(1+\kappa_{Q_{22}}^{2})}{(1+\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2})}\right)\left(\breve{c}_{20}U_{k_{2}}/d+\breve{c}_{21}\right)
. Thus we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\breve{c}d

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\breve{c}d.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.8
.

The proof for part (2) and (4) follow in the exact manner as part (1) and (3), respectively. Thus, to avoid repetition, we will only present proof for part (1) and (3).

1.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{i}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{i}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}\left\|y_{k}\right\|+A_{max}\left\|x_{k}\right\|\right]^{2}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b^{2}_{max}d+A^{2}_{max}\left(\mathbb{E}[\left\|x_{k}\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\left\|y_{k}\right\|^{2}]\right)}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}.

(Lemma
D.7
)

3.

Recall that
d_{k}^{x}=d_{k}^{xw}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})d_{k}^{xv}
. Using Part 1 of this lemma, we have

\displaystyle\|d_{k}^{x}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{k}^{xw}\|+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\left\|(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\right\|\|d_{k}^{xv}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.9
.

1.

Recall that
v_{k}=b_{1}(O_{k})-(A_{11}(O_{k})-A_{11})y_{k}-(A_{12}(O_{k})-A_{12})x_{k}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(\|b_{1}(O_{k})\|_{2}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[\|A_{11}(O_{k})-A_{11}\|^{2}\|y_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|A_{12}(O_{k})-A_{12}\|^{2}\|x_{k}\|^{2}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(b_{\max}^{2}d+4A_{max}^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3d\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right).

(Lemma
D.7
)

2.

This part follows in the exact manner as the previous one.

3.

Since
u_{k}=w_{k}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|w_{k}\|^{2}]+\frac{2\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\|(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\|^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|w_{k}\|^{2}]+\frac{2\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{k}\|^{2}]

(Lemma
D.3
)

\displaystyle\leq 3d\left(2+\frac{2\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right).

(Part 1 and 2 of this Lemma)

∎

Induction dependent lemmas

Lemma D.10
.

Assume at time
k
, Eqs.
D.4
,
D.5
and
D.6
are satisfied with
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},1\}=\hbar<\infty
. Then we have the following.

1.

\|\tilde{X}_{k}\|\leq\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{1}d+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{22}}\zeta_{k}^{x}
.

2.

\|\tilde{Y}_{k}\|\leq\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}
.

3.

\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]\leq\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}
.

4.

\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]\leq\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}
.

5.

\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}]\leq\underline{c}_{5}d^{2}\alpha_{k}+\underline{c}_{6}d\hbar\zeta_{k}^{x}
.

6.

\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k+1}\|^{2}]\leq\underline{c}_{7}d^{2}\beta_{k}+\underline{c}_{8}d\hbar\zeta_{k}^{y}
.

For an exact expression of the constants, refer to the proof of the lemma.

Lemma D.11
.

Consider
x_{k},y_{k}
as iterations generated by (
B.1
),
O_{k}
as Markovian noise in these iterations, and

as independent Markovian noise generated according to the stationary distribution of the Markov chain
\{O_{i}\}_{i\geq 0}
. Also, suppose that Eq.
D.4
,
D.5
and
