. Then, we have

1.

\|\mathbb{E}[F^{(i,j)}(O_{k+1},O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-F^{(i,j)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]\|\leq\hat{g}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\hat{g}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
.

2.

\mathbb{E}[F^{(i,j)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}
, where
\|\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}\|\leq\hat{g}_{3}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\hat{g}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
.

For an exact expression for the constants, please refer to the proof of this lemma.

,

\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{where}\penalty\ \penalty\ \|\check{R}^{u}_{k}\|\leq\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)^{2}\left(\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right)+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\varrho_{x}\left(\|\Gamma_{21}\|+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\|\Gamma_{11}\|\right)
.

For exact characterization of the constants please refer to the proof.

Lemma D.13
.

Assume at time
k>k_{0}
, where
k_{0}
is specified in the proof of Lemma
D.2
, Eqs.
D.4
,
D.5
and
D.6
are satisfied with
\max\{\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime xy}_{k}\|_{Q_{22}},\|\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\|_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}},1\}=\hbar<\infty
. Then, we have

\mathbb{E}[f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]=\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{yv}-d_{k+1}^{yv}+G^{(1,1)}_{k};\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{where}\penalty\ \penalty\ \|G^{(1,1)}_{k}\|\leq g_{1}d^{2}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+g_{2}d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}

2.

\mathbb{E}[f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]=\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv}+G^{(1,2)}_{k};\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{where}\penalty\ \penalty\ \|G^{(1,2)}_{k}\|\leq g_{3}d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{1.5}+\beta_{k})+g_{4}d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}

3.

\mathbb{E}[f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]=\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+d_{k}^{yw}-d_{k+1}^{yw}+G^{(2,1)}_{k};\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{where}\penalty\ \penalty\ \|G^{(2,1)}_{k}\|\leq g_{1}d^{2}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+g_{2}d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}

.

For exact characterization of the constants please refer to the proof.

Proof of induction dependent lemmas

Proof of Lemma
D.10
.

1.

Since have
\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}=\tilde{X}_{k}+\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{X}_{k}=\tilde{X}_{k}^{\prime}-\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})=\alpha_{k}\Sigma^{x}+R_{k},

where
R_{k}=\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{x}-\alpha_{k}(d_{k}^{x}+{d_{k}^{x}}^{\top})
.

Using Lemma
D.8
, we get

\displaystyle\|d_{k}^{x}\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\sqrt{\breve{c}d}

(Lemma
D.7
)

Hence,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{X}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}\|\Sigma^{x}\|+\|\tilde{C}^{\prime x}_{k}\|\zeta_{k}^{x}+2\alpha_{k}d\left(\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{1}d+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{22}}\zeta_{k}^{x},

where
\underline{c}_{1}=\sigma^{x}\tau_{mix}+2\left(\frac{2\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)\breve{c}_{f}\right)
.

2.

Since
\tilde{Y}_{k}=\tilde{Y}_{k}^{\prime}-\beta_{k}(d_{k}^{yv}+{d_{k}^{yv}}^{\top})=\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}+\tilde{C}^{\prime y}_{k}\zeta_{k}^{y}-\beta_{k}(d_{k}^{yv}+{d_{k}^{yv}}^{\top})
, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{Y}_{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}d\tau_{mix}\sigma^{y}+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}+2\beta_{k}\|d_{k}^{yv}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}d\tau_{mix}\sigma^{y}+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}\frac{4\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Lemma
D.8
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{k}d\tau_{mix}\sigma^{y}+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}+\beta_{k}\frac{4\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}\sqrt{\breve{c}d}

(Lemma
D.7
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}

where
\underline{c}_{2}=\sigma^{y}\tau_{mix}+\frac{4\sqrt{3\breve{c}}}{1-\rho}\breve{c}_{f}
.

3.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}-(L_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}+\|L_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2}\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d\|\tilde{X}_{k}\|+\|L_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2}d\|\tilde{Y}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2[d\|\tilde{X}_{k}\|+\|L_{k}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|^{2}d\|\tilde{Y}_{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d(\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{1}d+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{22}}\zeta_{k}^{x})+2d\varrho_{x}(\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d+\hbar\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y})

(Lemma
D.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}d^{2}\underline{c}_{3}+d\underline{c}_{4}\hbar\zeta_{k}^{x},

(Using
\zeta_{k}^{y}\leq\zeta_{k}^{x}
)

where
\underline{c}_{3}=2\underline{c}_{1}+\frac{2\beta}{\alpha}\underline{c}_{2}\varrho_{x}
and
\underline{c}_{4}=2\kappa_{Q_{22}}+2\varrho_{x}\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}
.

4.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]\leq d\|Y_{k}\|=d\|\tilde{Y}_{k}\|=\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}

5.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\alpha_{k}B_{22}^{k})\tilde{x}_{k}+\alpha_{k}u_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\alpha_{k}B_{22}^{k})\|^{2}\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}+\alpha_{k}^{2}\|u_{k}\|^{2}]

For the first term, recall that
B^{k}_{22}=\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})A_{12}+A_{22}
. Thus,
\|B^{k}_{22}\|\leq\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\|A_{12}\|+\|A_{22}\|
. In addition, from Lemma
D.10
, we have
\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]\leq\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}
. Furthermore, by lemma
D.9
we have
\mathbb{E}[\|u_{k}\|^{2}]\leq 6d\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
. Combining together the previous bounds, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}]\leq\underline{c}_{5}d^{2}\alpha_{k}+\underline{c}_{6}d\hbar\zeta_{k}^{x},

where
\underline{c}_{5}=2\underline{c}_{3}\left(1+\alpha\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\|A_{12}\|+\|A_{22}\|\right)^{2}\right)+12\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}^{2}\right)\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
and

\underline{c}_{6}=2\underline{c}_{4}\left(1+\alpha\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\|A_{12}\|+\|A_{22}\|\right)^{2}\right)
.

6.

From Eq. (
C.5
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\|(I-\beta_{k}B^{k}_{11}\tilde{y}_{k})+\beta_{k}A_{12}\tilde{x}_{k}+\beta_{k}v_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 3\mathbb{E}[\|I-\beta_{k}B^{k}_{11}\|^{2}\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|A_{12}\|^{2}\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|v_{k}\|^{2}]

Recall that
B^{k}_{11}=\Delta-A_{12}L_{k}
. Thus,
\|B^{k}_{11}\|\leq\|\Delta\|+\|A_{12}\|\kappa_{Q_{22}}=\varrho_{y}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k+1}\|^{2}]\leq

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}[2(1+\beta^{2}\varrho_{y}^{2})\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|A_{12}\|^{2}\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|v_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}[2(1+\beta^{2}\varrho_{y}^{2})\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\|A_{12}\|^{2}\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}+3\beta_{k}^{2}d\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}[2(1+\beta^{2}\varrho_{y}^{2})(\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y})+\beta_{k}^{2}\|A_{12}\|^{2}(\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x})

\displaystyle+3\beta_{k}^{2}d\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{c}_{7}d^{2}\beta_{k}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{8}\zeta_{k}^{y}

where
\underline{c}_{7}=6(1+\beta^{2}\varrho_{y}^{2})\underline{c}_{2}+\beta\|A_{12}\|^{2}\alpha\underline{c}_{3}+3\beta\left(b_{\max}^{2}+4A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)
and
\underline{c}_{8}=6(1+\beta^{2}\varrho_{y}^{2})\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\beta^{2}\|A_{12}\|^{2}\underline{c}_{4}
.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.11
.

1.

Recall that
F^{(i,j)}(O_{k+1},O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})=E\left[\hat{f}_{i}(O_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})f_{j}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})^{\top}\right]
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[F^{(i,j)}

\displaystyle(O_{k+1},O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-F^{(i,j)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{i}(O_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{j}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}-\left(\hat{f}_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]\bigg\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left(C_{i}(O_{k+1})-C_{i1}(O_{k+1})y_{k}-C_{i2}(O_{k+1})x_{k}\right)\left(b_{j}(O_{k})-(A_{j1}(O_{k})-A_{j1})y_{k}-(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})x_{k}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle-(C_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})-C_{i1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})y_{k}-C_{i2}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})x_{k})\left(b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})-(A_{j1}(\tilde{O}_{k})-A_{j1})y_{k}-(A_{j2}(\tilde{O}_{k})-A_{j2})x_{k}\right)^{\top}\bigg]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[C_{i}(O_{k+1})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}-C_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]\|+\|R_{k}\|,

where
R_{k}
includes all the remaining terms. Denote
\Lambda_{k}=(O_{k},O_{k+1})
and
\tilde{\Lambda}_{k}=(\tilde{O}_{k},\tilde{O}_{k+1})
. Clearly,
\Lambda_{k}
is a Markov chain, and
\tilde{\Lambda}_{k}
is another independent Markov chain following the stationary distribution of
\Lambda_{k}
. By definition of the function
C_{i}
and the mixing property of the Markov chain, we have

\displaystyle\max_{o,o^{\prime}}\|C_{i}(o^{\prime})b_{j}(o)^{\top}\|\leq

\displaystyle\max_{o}\|C_{i}(o)\|\max_{o}\|b_{j}(o)\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2b_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}.b_{max}\sqrt{d}

(Lemma
D.15
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2b_{max}^{2}}{(1-\rho)}d.

Hence, by geometric mixing of the Markov chain,
\|\mathbb{E}[C_{i}(O_{k+1})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}-C_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]\|
goes to zero geometrically fast. Hence,

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[C_{i}(O_{k+1})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}-C_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4b_{max}^{2}}{(1-\rho)}d\rho^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4b_{max}^{2}}{(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{\xi/2}{e\ln(1/\rho)}+K_{0}\right)^{\xi/2}d\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}.

(Lemma
D.19
)

For
R_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\|R_{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|]+\frac{4A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}[2\|x_{k}\|^{2}+2\|y_{k}\|^{2}+4\|x_{k}\|\|y_{k}\|]

(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|]+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}]

(AM-GM inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\left(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]}\right)+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}\right]

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\left(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\left(\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}+\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

Combining both the bounds together, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[F^{(i,j)}

\displaystyle(O_{k+1},O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-F^{(i,j)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]\|\leq\hat{g}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\hat{g}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}},

(
\zeta_{k}^{x}\leq\zeta_{k}^{y}
)

where
\hat{g}_{1}=\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}+\sqrt{\beta\underline{c}_{2}/\alpha})+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}(\underline{c}_{3}\sqrt{\alpha}+\beta\underline{c}_{2}/\sqrt{\alpha})+\frac{4b_{max}^{2}}{(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{\xi/2}{e\ln(1/\rho)}+K_{0}\right)^{\xi/2}
and
\hat{g}_{2}=\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}+\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}\right)+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\left(\underline{c}_{4}+\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\right)
.

2.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[F^{(i,j)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}{\left[\left(\sum_{l=k+1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})|\tilde{O}_{k+1}]}-C_{i2}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})x_{k}-C_{i1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})y_{k}\right)f_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})^{\top}\right]}

(Lemma
D.14
)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\sum_{l=k+1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})|\tilde{O}_{k+1}]}-C_{i2}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})x_{k}-C_{i1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1})y_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \left(b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})-(A_{j2}(\tilde{O}_{k})-A_{j2})x_{k}-(A_{j1}(\tilde{O}_{k})-A_{j1})y_{k}\right)^{\top}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}{\left[\sum_{l=k+1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}|\tilde{O}_{k+1}]}\right]}+\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=k+1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}|\tilde{O}_{k+1}]}]+\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}

(Fubini-Tonelli theorem)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=k+1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]}+\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}

(Tower Property)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k},

(Stationarity of
\tilde{O}_{k}
)

where
\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}
represents the remainder terms. Using the exact arguments as in the previous part, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|]+\frac{4A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}[2\|x_{k}\|^{2}+2\|y_{k}\|^{2}+4\|x_{k}\|\|y_{k}\|]

(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|]+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}]

(AM-GM inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\left(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]}\right)+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}\right]

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{d}\left(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}\left(\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}+\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq\hat{g}_{3}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\hat{g}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}},

(
\zeta_{k}^{x}\leq\zeta_{k}^{y}
)

where
\hat{g}_{3}=\frac{8b_{max}A_{max}}{1-\rho}(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}+\sqrt{\beta\underline{c}_{2}/\alpha})+\frac{16A_{max}^{2}}{1-\rho}(\underline{c}_{3}\sqrt{\alpha}+\beta\underline{c}_{2}/\sqrt{\alpha})
.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.12
.

Assume that
\psi_{k}^{i}=b_{i}(O_{k})-(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}-(A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})x_{k}
for
i\in\{1,2\}
. Note that
\psi_{k}^{(1)}=v_{k}
and
\psi_{k}^{(2)}=w_{k}
. For arbitrary
i,j\in\{1,2\}
We have:

\displaystyle\psi_{k}^{(i)}{\psi_{k}^{(j)}}^{\top}=

\displaystyle b_{i}(O_{k})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}-(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}-(A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})x_{k}b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}

\displaystyle-b_{i}(O_{k})y_{k}^{\top}(A_{j1}(O_{k})-A_{j1})^{\top}+(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}(A_{j1}(O_{k})-A_{j1})^{\top}

\displaystyle+(A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})x_{k}y_{k}^{\top}(A_{j1}(O_{k})-A_{j1})^{\top}-b_{i}(O_{k})x_{k}^{\top}(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})^{\top}

\displaystyle+(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}x_{k}^{\top}(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})^{\top}+(A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})x_{k}x_{k}^{\top}(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})^{\top}.

We will analyze each term separately and use
[
KMN+20
, Lemma 23]
extensively without stating to decompose the expectation of the outer product of two random vectors.

•

Let
\tilde{O}_{k}
be a Markov chain with starting distribution as stationary distribution. Then:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]+\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{ij}+\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}].

We have

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{o}\|b_{i}(o)b_{j}(o)^{\top}\|\max_{o^{\prime}}d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o^{\prime})||\mu(\cdot))

\displaystyle\leq\max_{o}\|b_{i}(o)b_{j}(o)^{\top}\|\rho^{k}

\displaystyle\leq b_{max}^{2}d\rho^{k},

where the second inequality is due to the geometric mixing of the Markov chain stated in Remark
Remark
.

Using Lemma
D.19
,
\rho^{k}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\left(\frac{\xi}{2e\log(1/\rho)}+K_{0}\right)^{\xi/2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}
. Hence, we have
\|\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}]-\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{k})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{k})^{\top}]\|\leq\frac{b_{max}^{2}d}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\left(\frac{\xi}{2e\log(1/\rho)}+K_{0}\right)^{\xi/2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}
for all
k>0
.

•

For the
5th
term, we have the following:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}(A_{j1}(O_{k})-A_{j1})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{2}_{max}\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}\|]

\displaystyle=4A^{2}_{max}\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{2}_{max}\left(\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

•

For the
9th
term, we shall do the following:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[(A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})x_{k}x_{k}^{\top}(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{2}_{max}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}x_{k}^{\top}\|]

\displaystyle=4A^{2}_{max}\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{2}_{max}\left(\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}\right).

(Lemma
D.10
)

•

For the
2nd
and
4th
terms:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}b_{j}(O_{k})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|b_{j}(O_{k})\|^{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq 2A_{max}b_{max}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|y_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq 2A_{max}b_{max}\left(\sqrt{\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq 2A_{max}b_{max}\left(d\sqrt{\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}}+\hbar\sqrt{d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

where the last inequality is by
\hbar\geq 1
.

Similarly for the 4
th
term.

•

For the
3rd
and
7th
terms:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[b_{i}(O_{k})

\displaystyle x_{k}^{\top}(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|b_{i}(O_{k})\|^{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|(A_{i2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})x_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq 2A_{max}b_{max}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|x_{k}\|^{2}]}

\displaystyle\leq 2A_{max}b_{max}\left(d\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}}+\hbar\sqrt{d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

Similarly for the
7th
term.

•

For the
6th
and
8th
terms:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}x_{k}^{\top}(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{i2})^{\top}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|(A_{i1}(O_{k})-A_{i1})y_{k}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|(A_{j2}(O_{k})-A_{j2})x_{k}\|^{2}]

(Young’s Inequality)

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{2}_{max}\left(\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}+\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{2}_{max}d^{2}\alpha_{k}\left(\underline{c}_{2}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\underline{c}_{3}\right)+4\hbar dA_{max}^{2}\left(\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\underline{c}_{4}\right)\zeta_{k}^{x}

(
\zeta_{k}^{y}\leq\zeta_{k}^{x}
)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\psi_{k}^{(i)}{\psi_{k}^{(j)}}^{\top}\right]=\Gamma_{ij}+\check{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}

where
\|\check{R}^{(i,j)}_{k}\|\leq\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
. Here

\displaystyle\check{c}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{b_{max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\left(\frac{\xi}{2e\log(1/\rho)}+K_{0}\right)^{\xi/2}+12A_{max}^{2}\sqrt{\alpha}\left(\underline{c}_{2}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\underline{c}_{3}\right)+4A_{max}b_{max}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\underline{c}_{2}}+\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\check{c}_{2}

\displaystyle=12A_{max}^{2}\left(\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}+\underline{c}_{4}\right)+4A_{max}b_{max}\left(\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}+\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}\right).

This proves the part
1
of the Lemma.

For the last part,
\mathbb{E}[u_{k}u_{k}^{\top}]
, we have:
Given that
u_{k}=w_{k}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}
:

\displaystyle u_{k}u_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=w_{k}w_{k}^{\top}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}w_{k}v_{k}^{\top}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})^{\top}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}w_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\right)^{2}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}v_{k}^{\top}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})^{\top}

We will again analyse each term separately.

•

\mathbb{E}[w_{k}w_{k}^{\top}]=\Gamma_{22}+\check{R}^{(2,2)}_{k};\penalty\ \penalty\ \text{where}\penalty\ \penalty\ \|\check{R}^{(2,2)}_{k}\|\leq\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
.

•

\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\|\mathbb{E}[w_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]\|\|(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})^{\top}\|\leq\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\varrho_{x}\left(\|\Gamma_{21}\|+\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right)

•

\left(\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\right)^{2}\|(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\|\|\mathbb{E}[v_{k}v_{k}^{\top}]\|(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})^{\top}\|\leq\left(\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\right)^{2}\varrho_{x}^{2}\Big(\|\Gamma_{11}\|+\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\Big)

Hence,

\mathbb{E}[u_{k}u_{k}^{\top}]=\Gamma_{22}+\check{R}_{k}^{u},

where
\|\check{R}_{k}^{u}\|\leq\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}\right)^{2}\left(\check{c}_{1}d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\check{c}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right)+\frac{\beta_{k}}{\alpha_{k}}\varrho_{x}\left(\|\Gamma_{21}\|+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\|\Gamma_{11}\|\varrho_{x}\right)
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
D.13
.

The results in part (3) and (4) of this Lemma follow in exactly same manner as part (1) and (2), respectively. Hence, we only present proof for the first two parts to avoid repetition.

1.

By definition, we had
v_{k}=f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})
. By Remark
Remark
, we have a unique function
\hat{f}_{1}(o,x_{k},y_{k})
such that

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{1}(o,x_{k},y_{k})=f_{1}(o,x_{k},y_{k})+\sum_{o^{\prime}\in S}P(o^{\prime}|o)\hat{f}_{1}(o^{\prime},x_{k},y_{k})

where
P(o^{\prime}|o)
is the transition probability corresponding to the Markov chain
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]

(D.31)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\sum_{o^{\prime}\in S}P(o^{\prime}|O_{k})\hat{f}_{1}(o^{\prime},x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})+\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}-\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})\right)\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}+\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}-\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle d_{k}^{yv}-d_{k+1}^{yv}+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}-\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top})}_{T_{2}}\bigg]

For
T_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|T_{1}\|]\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\mathbb{E}[(\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|+\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|).\|\tilde{y}_{k+1}\|]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[(A_{max}\|x_{k}\|+A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+b_{max}\sqrt{d}).\|\tilde{y}_{k+1}\|]

(Eq. (
B.1
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[(A_{max}\|x_{k}\|+A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+b_{max}\sqrt{d})^{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k+1}\|^{2}]}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[A_{max}^{2}(\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2})+b_{max}^{2}d]}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{7}}d\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\sqrt{\underline{c}_{8}d\hbar\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{7}}d^{1.5}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+d\hbar\sqrt{\underline{c}_{8}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right),

(Lemma
D.7
)

In addition, using the Eq. (
C.6
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{2}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(\tilde{y}_{k+1}^{\top}-\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\left(-\beta_{k}B_{11}^{k}\tilde{y}_{k}-\beta_{k}A_{12}\tilde{x}_{k}+\beta_{k}v_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)v_{k}^{\top}\right]}_{T_{21}}

\displaystyle-\beta_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\left(B_{11}^{k}\tilde{y}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]}_{T_{22}}-\beta_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\left(A_{12}\tilde{x}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]}_{T_{23}}.

•

For
T_{21}
, denote
\tilde{O}
as the random variable with distribution coming from the stationary distribution of the Markov chain
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]

(Tower property)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[F^{(1,1)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]+\mathbb{E}[F^{(1,1)}(O_{k+1},O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]-\mathbb{E}[F^{(1,1)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]

Using Part (2) on the first term and Part (1) on the second term of Lemma
D.11
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\hat{R}_{k}^{(1,1)}

(Lemma
D.11
)

where
\left\|\hat{R}_{k}^{(1,1)}\right\|\leq(\hat{g}_{1}+\hat{g}_{3})d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+2\hat{g}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
.

•

For
T_{22}
, we have

\displaystyle\|T_{22}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|\|B_{11}^{k}\|\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\varrho_{y}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}\left(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|\right)\right)\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|\right]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3}\varrho_{y}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[b_{max}^{2}d+A_{max}^{2}\left(\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}\right)\right]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\varrho_{y}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{y}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Lemma
D.7
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\varrho_{y}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\beta_{k}\underline{c}_{2}d^{2}+\hbar d\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}\zeta_{k}^{y}}

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\varrho_{y}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{2}}d\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\hbar\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}d}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right).

•

For
T_{23}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{23}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right\|\left\|A_{12}\|\|\tilde{x}_{k}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\|A_{12}\|}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}\left(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|\right)\right)\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|\right]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3}\|A_{12}\|}{1-\rho}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[b_{max}^{2}d+A_{max}^{2}\left(\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}\right)\right]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\|A_{12}\|}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Lemma
D.7
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\|A_{12}\|}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}}

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\|A_{12}\|}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}d\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+\hbar\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}d}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right).

Note that
\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}\geq\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\beta_{k}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}
and
\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\geq\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\beta_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
. Hence,

\displaystyle T_{2}=\beta_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+R_{k}^{1},

where
\|R_{k}^{2}\|\leq d^{2}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\left(\hat{g}_{1}+\hat{g}_{3}\right)+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{y}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{2}}+\|A_{12}\|\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}\right)\right)+d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\\
\times\left(2\hat{g}_{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3d}}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\varrho_{y}\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}+\|A_{12}\|\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}\right)\right)
.

Combining the bounds for
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{k}\tilde{y}_{k}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=d_{k}^{yv}-d_{k+1}^{yv}+\beta_{k}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{i}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{j}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+G_{k}^{(1,1)}

where
\left\|G_{k}^{(1,1)}\right\|\leq g_{1}d^{2}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+g_{2}d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{y}}
. Here

\displaystyle g_{1}

\displaystyle=d^{2}\left(\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3\underline{c}_{7}}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{y}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{2}}+\|A_{12}\|\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}\right)\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}(\hat{g}_{1}+\hat{g}_{3})\right),

\displaystyle g_{2}

\displaystyle=d\left(\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3\underline{c}_{8}}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{1-\rho}\left(\varrho_{y}\sqrt{\kappa_{Q_{\Delta,\beta}}}+\|A_{12}\|\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}\right)\right)+\frac{2\beta}{\alpha}\hat{g}_{2}\right).

2.

By definition, we had
v_{k}=f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})
. By Remark
Remark
, we have a unique function
\hat{f}_{1}(o,x_{k},y_{k})
such that

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{1}(o,x_{k},y_{k})=f_{1}(o,x_{k},y_{k})+\sum_{o^{\prime}\in S}P(o^{\prime}|o)\hat{f}_{1}(o^{\prime},x_{k},y_{k})

where
P(o^{\prime}|o)
is the transition probability corresponding to the Markov chain
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{k}\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[f_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}]

(D.32)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\sum_{o^{\prime}\in S}P(o^{\prime}|O_{k})\hat{f}_{1}(o^{\prime},x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})+\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k-1}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top}-\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})\right)\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}+\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}-\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv}

(D.33)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k+1},y_{k+1})-\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}}_{T_{3}}+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}-\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top})}_{T_{4}}\bigg]

For
T_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|T_{3}\|]\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\mathbb{E}[(\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|+\|y_{k+1}-y_{k}\|).\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[(A_{max}\|x_{k}\|+A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+b_{max}\sqrt{d}).\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|]

(Eq. (
B.1
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[(A_{max}\|x_{k}\|+A_{max}\|y_{k}\|+b_{max}\sqrt{d})^{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k+1}\|^{2}]}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[A_{max}^{2}(\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2})+b_{max}^{2}d]}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{5}}d\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\sqrt{\underline{c}_{6}d\hbar\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right)

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3}\alpha_{k}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{5}}d^{1.5}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+d\hbar\sqrt{\underline{c}_{6}\zeta_{k}^{y}}\right),

(Lemma
D.7
)

In addition, using the Eq. (
C.6
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{4}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(\tilde{x}_{k+1}^{\top}-\tilde{x}_{k}^{\top})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\left(-\alpha_{k}(B_{22}^{k}\tilde{x}_{k})+\alpha_{k}w_{k}+\beta_{k}(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)w_{k}^{\top}\right]}_{T_{41}}

\displaystyle-\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\left(B_{22}^{k}\tilde{x}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]}_{T_{42}}+\beta_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)\left((L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})v_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]}_{T_{43}}

•

For
T_{41}
, denote
\tilde{O}
as the random variable with distribution coming from the stationary distribution of the Markov chain
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]

(Tower property)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(O_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\hat{f}_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[F^{(1,2)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]+\mathbb{E}[F^{(1,2)}(O_{k+1},O_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]-\mathbb{E}[F^{(1,2)}(\tilde{O}_{k+1},\tilde{O}_{k},x_{k},y_{k})]

Using Part (2) on the first term and Part (1) on the second term of Lemma
D.11
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{l})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+\hat{R}_{k}^{(1,2)}

(Lemma
D.11
)

where
\left\|\hat{R}_{k}^{(1,2)}\right\|\leq(\hat{g}_{1}+\hat{g}_{3})d^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+2\hat{g}_{2}d\hbar\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
.

•

For
T_{42}
, we have

\displaystyle\|T_{42}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\|\|B_{22}^{k}\|\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}\left(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|\right)\right)\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|\right]

(Lemma
D.4
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[b_{max}^{2}d+A_{max}^{2}\left(\|x_{k}\|^{2}+\|y_{k}\|^{2}\right)\right]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{x}_{k}\|^{2}]}

(Lemma
D.7
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3d}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}\underline{c}_{3}d^{2}+\hbar d\underline{c}_{4}\zeta_{k}^{x}}

(Lemma
D.10
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{3}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}d^{1.5}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}+d\hbar\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}\right).

•

For
T_{43}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{43}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right\|\left\|(L_{k+1}+A_{22}^{-1}A_{21})\|\|v_{k}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|))\left\|v_{k}\right\|\right]

(Lemma
D.14
and Lemma
D.3
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}\mathbb{E}\left[(b_{max}\sqrt{d}+A_{max}(\|x_{k}\|+\|y_{k}\|))^{2}+\left\|v_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

(AM-GM inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3d\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}\left(2b_{max}^{2}+5A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)

(Lemma
D.7
and Lemma
D.9
)

Hence,

\displaystyle T_{4}=\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+R_{k}^{2},

where
\|R_{k}^{2}\|\leq\left(\hat{g}_{1}+\hat{g}_{3}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}\right)d^{2}\alpha_{k}^{1.5}+\beta_{k}\frac{3d\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}\left(2b_{max}^{2}+5A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)+\left(2\hat{g}_{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}\right)d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
.

Combining the bounds for
T_{3}
and
T_{4}
and using
\zeta_{k}^{y}\leq\zeta_{k}^{x}
, we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{O_{k}}\hat{f}_{1}(\cdot,x_{k},y_{k})\right)(f_{2}(O_{k},x_{k},y_{k}))^{\top}\right]=d_{k}^{xv}-d_{k+1}^{xv}+\alpha_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b_{1}(\tilde{O}_{j})b_{2}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}]}+G^{(1,2)}_{k}

where
\left\|G^{(1,2)}_{k}\right\|\leq g_{3}d^{2}(\alpha_{k}^{1.5}+\beta_{k})+g_{4}d\hbar\alpha_{k}\sqrt{\zeta_{k}^{x}}
. Here

\displaystyle g_{3}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigg\{\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{\frac{3\alpha\underline{c}_{5}}{\beta}}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{3}}\right)+\hat{g}_{1}+\hat{g}_{3},

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \quad\quad\frac{3\varrho_{x}}{1-\rho}\left(2b_{max}^{2}+5A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}\right)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle g_{4}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{b_{max}^{2}+A_{max}^{2}\breve{c}}\left(\check{h}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)\sqrt{3\underline{c}_{5}}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\varrho_{x}+\|A_{22}\|\right)}{1-\rho}\sqrt{\underline{c}_{4}}\right)+2\hat{g}_{2}.

∎

Additional Lemmas

Lemma D.14
.

[
DMPS18
, Proposition 21.2.3]

Consider a finite state space Markov chain with the set of state space as
S
and let
\mu(\cdot)
denote the stationary distribution. For any
o\in S
and arbitrary
x
and
y
define
f(o,x,y)=b(o)-(A_{1}(o))x-(A_{2}(o))y
such that
\sum_{o\in S}\mu(o)f(o)=0
. Then one of the solutions for Poisson equation is given by:

\displaystyle\hat{f}(o,x,y)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{\left[f(O_{k},x,y)|O_{0}=o\right]}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[b(O_{k})|O_{o}=o]}-\left(\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{1}(O_{k}))|O_{0}=o]}\right)x-\left(\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}{[A_{2}(O_{k}))|O_{0}=o]}\right)y,

where each infinite summation is finite for all
o\in S
.

Lemma D.15
.

Consider an Ergodic Markov chain
\{O_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
with the transition probability
P(\cdot|\cdot)
and the stationary distribution
\mu
and let
\rho
be the mixing rate of this Markov chain. Consider the functions
h_{1},h_{2},h_{3}:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
for arbitrary integers
d_{1}
and
d_{2}
. For all
o\in\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[h_{1}(O_{k})-h_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\Big|O_{0}=o\right]\right\|\leq\frac{2}{1-\rho}\max_{o\in\mathcal{S}}\left\|h_{1}(o)\right\|,

where
\{\tilde{O}_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
is an independent stationary Markov chain.

Furthermore, if
\mathbb{E}[h_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})]=0,\penalty\ \forall\penalty\ k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[h_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})h_{3}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{1}{1-\rho}\max_{o\in\mathcal{S}}\left\|h_{2}(o)\right\|\max_{o\in\mathcal{S}}\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|.

Proof of Lemma
D.15
.

An Ergodic Markov chain enjoys an exponential mixing property
[
LP17
]
, that is, for all
o\in\mathcal{S}
, we have
d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o)||\mu(\cdot))\leq\rho^{k}
for some
\rho\in[0,1)
.

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[h_{1}(O_{k})-h_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})\Big|O_{0}=o\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[[h_{1}(O_{k})-h_{1}(\tilde{O}_{k})]\Big|O_{0}=o\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)-\mu(o^{\prime}))h_{1}(o^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)-\mu(o^{\prime})\right|\left\|h_{1}(o^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\max_{o^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\left\|h_{1}(o^{\prime\prime})\right\|\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)-\mu(o^{\prime})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\max_{o^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\left\|h_{1}(o^{\prime\prime})\right\|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o)||\mu(\cdot)).

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o)|\mu(\cdot))\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\rho^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\rho}

The first result follows by combining the inequalities.

For the second part, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[h_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})h_{3}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[h_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})h_{3}(\tilde{O}_{0})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\max_{o}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[h_{2}(\tilde{O}_{k})|\tilde{O}_{0}=o\right]\right\|\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\max_{o}\left\|\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)h_{2}(o^{\prime})\right\|\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\max_{o}\left\|\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)-\mu(o^{\prime}))h_{2}(o^{\prime})\right\|\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\max_{o}\sum_{o^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}|P^{k}(o^{\prime}|o)-\mu(o^{\prime})|\left\|h_{2}(o^{\prime})\right\|\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\max_{o}\left\|h_{2}(o)\right\|\max_{o}\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|\max_{o^{\prime}}d_{TV}(P^{k}(\cdot|o^{\prime})||\mu(\cdot))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\rho}\max_{o}\left\|h_{2}(o)\right\|\max_{o}\left\|h_{3}(o)\right\|

∎

Lemma D.16
.

Consider a Hurwitz matrix
A
, a symmetric
\Sigma
, and the solution
P
to the Lyapunov equation
AP+PA^{\top}=\Sigma
. We have

\displaystyle\|P\|\leq\|\Sigma\|\|U\|\|U^{-1}\|\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m}{n+n^{\prime}\choose n}\frac{1}{(-2r)^{n+n^{\prime}+1}},

where
U
is the generalized eigen vector of
A
, and
m
is the largest algebraic multiplicity of the matrix
A
and
r=\max_{i}\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]
, where
\lambda_{i}
is the
i
-th eigen value.

Proof of Lemma
D.16
.

We know that the solution of the Lyapunov function
AP+PA^{\top}=\Sigma
can be written as
P=\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{A\tau}\Sigma e^{A^{\top}\tau}d\tau
. Hence,

\displaystyle\|P\|=

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{A\tau}\Sigma e^{A^{\top}\tau}d\tau\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\Sigma\|\int_{0}^{\infty}\|e^{A\tau}\|^{2}d\tau.

Consider the Jordan canonical form of
A
as
A=UJU^{-1}
. Then we have
e^{A\tau}=Ue^{J\tau}U^{-1}
, and hence
\|e^{A\tau}\|\leq\|U\|\|U^{-1}\|\|e^{J\tau}\|
. But we know that
\|e^{J\tau}\|\leq\max_{i}e^{r_{i}\tau}\sum_{n=0}^{m_{i}}\tau^{n}/n!\leq\max_{i}e^{r_{i}\tau}\max_{i}\sum_{n=0}^{m_{i}}\tau^{n}/n!=e^{r\tau}\sum_{n=0}^{m}\tau^{n}/n!
. Here
r_{i}=\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]
, where
\lambda_{i}
is the
i
-th eigen value and
m_{i}
is its algebraic multiplicity. In addition,
r=\max_{i}r_{i}<0
and
m=\max_{i}m_{i}
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\|e^{A\tau}\|^{2}d\tau\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{2r\tau}\left[\sum_{n=0}^{m}\tau^{n}/n!\right]^{2}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{2r\tau}\tau^{n+n^{\prime}}/(n!n^{\prime}!)d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m}\frac{1}{-2r}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-z}(-z/2r)^{n+n^{\prime}}/(n!n^{\prime}!)dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m}\frac{1}{(-2r)^{n+n^{\prime}+1}\times n!n^{\prime}!}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-z}z^{n+n^{\prime}+1-1}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m}\frac{(n+n^{\prime})!}{(-2r)^{n+n^{\prime}+1}\times n!n^{\prime}!}=\sum_{n,n^{\prime}=0}^{m}{n+n^{\prime}\choose n}\frac{1}{(-2r)^{n+n^{\prime}+1}}

∎

Lemma D.17
.

Consider the recursion

\displaystyle L^{\prime}(I-bB_{11})

\displaystyle=(I-aA_{22})L+bA_{22}^{-1}A_{21}B_{11}.

where
a
and
b
are some arbitrary constants,
B_{11}=\Delta-A_{12}L
, and
A_{22}
is a Hurwitz matrix. Assume that the constants
a
and
b
satisfy

\frac{b}{a}\leq\frac{a_{22}/2}{(\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}+1)(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}})}

b\leq\frac{1}{2(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}})\kappa_{Q_{22}}};\penalty\ \penalty\ a\leq\frac{1}{2\|Q_{22}\|\|A_{22}\|^{2}_{Q_{22}}}.

If
\|L\|_{Q_{22}}\leq 1
, then

\displaystyle L^{\prime}

\displaystyle=(I-aA_{22})L+bD(L).

where
D(L)=(A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}+(I-aA_{22})L)B_{11}(I-bB_{11})^{-1}
. Furthermore,
\|L^{\prime}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq 1
and
\|D(L)\|_{Q_{22}}\leq c_{D}=2(\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}+1)(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}})
.

Proof of Lemma
D.17
.

By definition, we have
\|B_{11}\|_{Q_{22}}=\|\Delta-A_{12}L\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}}
. Thus, by the assumption
b
, we have
\sqrt{\frac{\gamma_{\max}(Q_{22})}{\gamma_{\min}(Q_{22})}}b\|B_{11}\|_{Q_{22}}\leq\frac{1}{2}
which implies
b\|B_{11}\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Thus,
I-bB_{11}
is invertible and we have,

\displaystyle L^{\prime}

\displaystyle=((I-aA_{22})L+bA_{22}^{-1}A_{21}B_{11})(I-bB_{11})^{-1}

\displaystyle=(I-aA_{22})L+bD(L)

where
D(L)=(A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}+(I-aA_{22})L)B_{11}(I-bB_{11})^{-1}
. Recall due to the assumption on
b
,
\|I-bB_{11}\|_{Q_{22}}\geq 1/2
, which implies that
\|D(L)\|_{Q_{22}}\leq 2(\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}+1)\|B_{11}\|_{Q_{22}}
. Thus, we have,

\displaystyle\|L^{\prime}\|_{Q_{22}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-a_{22}a)\|L\|_{Q_{22}}+b\|D(L)\|_{Q_{22}}

(Lemma
D.21
)

\displaystyle\leq(1-a_{22}a)\|L\|_{Q_{22}}+a_{22}a\left(\frac{b}{a_{22}a}\|D(L)\|_{Q_{22}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-a_{22}a)+a_{22}a\left(\frac{2b}{a_{22}a}(\|A_{22}^{-1}A_{21}\|_{Q_{22}}+1)(\|\Delta\|_{Q_{22}}+\|A_{12}\|_{Q_{22}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1.

∎

Lemma D.18
.

For any
\xi\geq 0
, and for all
n\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{\xi}}-\frac{1}{(n+1)^{\xi}}\leq\frac{\xi}{n^{\xi+1}}.

Proof of Lemma
D.18
.

Define the function
f(x)=\frac{1}{(x+n)^{\xi}}
. By Taylor’s theorem, for
x\in[0,1]
, and for some
z\in[0,x]
, we have

\displaystyle f(x)=f(0)+f^{\prime}(z)x=\frac{1}{n^{\xi}}-\frac{x\xi}{(n+z)^{\xi+1}}.

Hence, by choosing
x=1
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{\xi}}-\frac{1}{(n+1)^{\xi}}=\frac{\xi}{(n+z)^{\xi+1}}\leq\frac{\xi}{n^{\xi+1}}

∎

Lemma D.19
.

For any
\xi\in(0,1)
,
\rho<1
, and
n\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\rho^{x}(x+n)^{\xi}\leq\left(\frac{\xi}{e\ln(1/\rho)}+n\right)^{\xi}\penalty\ \forall x\geq 0.

Proof of Lemma
D.19
.

\displaystyle\rho^{x}(x+n)^{\xi}

\displaystyle=(\rho^{\frac{x}{\xi}}x+\rho^{\frac{x}{\xi}}n)^{\xi}.

Since
x\geq 0
and
\rho<1
, we can bound the second term by
n
. For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\rho^{\frac{x}{\xi}}x=e^{\frac{x}{\xi}\ln(\rho)}x.

The maximum value of this function is
\frac{\xi}{e\ln(1/\rho)}
which is achieved at
x=\frac{\xi}{\ln(1/\rho)}
. Combining the above two bounds, we get

\displaystyle\rho^{x}(x+n)^{\xi}\leq\left(\frac{\xi}{e\ln(1/\rho)}+n\right)^{\xi}\penalty\ \forall x\geq 0.

∎

Lemma D.20
.

For any symmetric matrix
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, we have

\displaystyle trace(X)\leq d\|X\|.

Proof of Lemma
D.20
.

By eigenvalue decomposion of
X
, we have
X=\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda^{\top}
. Taking the trace of
X
, we have
trace(X)=trace(\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda^{\top})=trace(\Sigma\Lambda\Lambda^{\top})=trace(\Sigma)=\sum_{i}\sigma_{i}\leq d\sigma_{\max}=d\|X\|
.
∎

Lemma D.21
.

Suppose
-A
is a Hurwitz matrix. Define
Q
to be the solution to Lyapunov equation,

A^{\top}Q+QA=I

Then for all
\epsilon\in[0,\frac{1}{2\|Q\|\|A\|_{Q}^{2}}]

\|I-\epsilon A\|_{Q}^{2}\leq(1-a\epsilon),\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \text{where }a=\frac{1}{2\|Q\|}.

Proof of Lemma
D.21
.

Using the definition of matrix norm we have:

\displaystyle\|I-\epsilon A\|_{Q}^{2}

\displaystyle=\max_{\|x\|_{Q}=1}x^{\top}(I-\epsilon A)^{\top}Q(I-\epsilon A)x

\displaystyle=\max_{\|x\|_{Q}=1}\left(x^{\top}Qx-\epsilon x^{\top}(A^{\top}Q+QA)x+\epsilon^{2}x^{\top}A^{\top}QAx\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1-\epsilon\min_{\|x\|_{Q}=1}\|x\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\max_{\|x\|_{Q}=1}\|Ax\|_{Q}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\epsilon\frac{1}{\|Q\|}+\epsilon^{2}\|A\|_{Q}^{2}.

For any
\epsilon\in\left[0,\frac{1}{2\|Q\|\|A\|_{Q}^{2}}\right]
, we have:

\displaystyle\|I-\epsilon A\|_{Q}^{2}\leq 1-\frac{\epsilon}{2\|Q\|}.

∎

dependence while the matrix
\ell_{2}

Appendix F

Simulation details for Fig.
1(a)

For simulation,consider a
1
-d linear SA with
|S|=2
for Markovian noise. The transition probability is given by:

\displaystyle P=\begin{bmatrix}5/8&3/8\\
3/4&1/4\end{bmatrix},\penalty\ \mu=[2/3,1/3]

The update matrices (in 1-d case scalars) were chosen as the following:

\displaystyle A_{11}(1)

\displaystyle=-0.5;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{11}(2)=-2;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{11}=-1

\displaystyle A_{12}(1)

\displaystyle=-1;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{12}(2)=-1;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{12}=-1

\displaystyle A_{21}(1)

\displaystyle=2.5;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{21}(2)=1;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{21}=2

\displaystyle A_{22}(1)

\displaystyle=0;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{22}(2)=3;\penalty\ \penalty\ A_{22}=1

\displaystyle b_{1}(1)

\displaystyle=-3/2;\penalty\ \penalty\ b_{1}(2)=3;\penalty\ \penalty\ b_{1}=0

\displaystyle b_{2}(1)

\displaystyle=3;\penalty\ \penalty\ b_{2}(2)=-6;\penalty\ \penalty\ b_{2}=0

For the step size,
\alpha=1
and
\beta=1
. Observe that

and therefore

is Hurwitz. We sample

and

uniformly from
[-5,5]
. The bold lines are the mean across five sample paths, whereas the shaded region is the standard deviation from the mean path. The plots start from
0.1
instead of
0
. This is done intentionally so that the initial randomness dies down.

Appendix G
Discussion on the best choice of step size

Consider the linear SA (
4.6a
). In order to get a faster convergence suppose that we run the second time-scale
y_{k+1}=(1-\beta_{k})y_{k}+\beta_{k}x_{k}
where
\beta_{k}=\frac{\beta}{k}
. Notice that with the choice of
\beta=1
, we again derive the Polyak-Ruppert averaging iterate (
4.6b
). An interesting question to investigate is why the optimal choice of
\beta
is equal to
1
.

According to Theorem
4.1
, the leading term in the convergence of
\mathbb{E}[y_{k}y_{k}^{\top}]
is
\beta_{k}\Sigma^{y}
. Furthermore, by (
4.4c
) we have
\Sigma^{y}=(\Gamma^{y}+\Sigma^{x}A_{22}^{-T}+A_{22}^{-1}\Sigma^{x})/(2-\beta^{-1})
. Hence, to find
\beta
that minimizes the norm of
\Sigma^{y}
, we need to choose
\beta
which minimizes
h(\beta)=\beta^{2}/(2\beta-1)
. The plot of the function
h(\beta)
is shown in Figure
5
. Clearly, this function is minimized at
\beta=1
, and hence the Polyak-Ruppert averaging is optimal.

Figure 5
:
The function
h(\beta)=\frac{\beta^{2}}{2\beta-1}
